(dynamic workflow)","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"design","description":"Grant or revoke Claude agent access to your Design projects","argumentHint":"consent | revoke","builtin":true},{"name":"design-sync","description":"Push a React design system to claude.ai/design. This runs a converter that bundles the real component code (from Storybook or a bare package) and uploads it. Use when the user runs /design-sync or says \"sync my design system to Claude Design\".","argumentHint":"[<project hint, e.g. \"Acme DS\">]","builtin":true},{"name":"dataviz","description":"Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. When the destination is a first-party document connector (host-designated, never self-described) that renders live charts, hand it the rows (inline, or as an uploaded data file the chart cites) rather than a rendered PNG/SVG — a picture of a chart loses hover, data inspection and per-value comments. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"update-config","description":"Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"verify","description":"Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"debug","description":"Enable debug logging for this session and help diagnose issues","argumentHint":"[issue description]","builtin":true},{"name":"code-review","description":"Review the current diff, or a PR number/branch/path target, for correctness bugs (plus reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups where the model's review recipe covers them) at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud); with no level given, it reuses the level you typed last. Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review. For ultra on a GitHub.com PR target, --post asks to post the finished review’s findings to the PR as a single comment from the user’s GitHub account (not a review; the launch dialog still confirms in interactive sessions, while non-interactive mode posts on the flag alone) and --no-post hides that option.","argumentHint":"[low|medium|high|xhigh|max|ultra] [--fix] [--comment] [<pr#>|<branch>|<path>]","aliases":["review"],"builtin":true},{"name":"simplify","description":"Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.","argumentHint":"[<target>]","builtin":true},{"name":"batch","description":"Research and plan a large-scale change, then execute it in parallel across 5–30 isolated worktree agents that each open a PR.","argumentHint":"<instruction>","builtin":true},{"name":"fewer-permission-prompts","description":"Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"doctor","description":"Health-check the user's Claude Code setup and fix issues: diagnose installation health — what the `claude doctor` terminal diagnostics cover — from local data (duplicate or leftover installs, PATH, unparseable settings files, broken or colliding agent definitions, skills whose frontmatter fails to parse); find unused skills, MCP servers, and plugins versus their context cost and disable dead weight; deduplicate local CLAUDE.md files against checked-in ones; trim checked-in CLAUDE.md files by cutting content a session could derive from the codebase (directory layouts, tech-stack lists, architecture overviews) while keeping gotchas, rationale, and non-standard conventions; migrate always-loaded CLAUDE.md guidance into lazy skills and nested CLAUDE.md files; flag slow hooks and context-heavy extensions; check the installed version is current; make auto mode the default permission mode; and pre-approve frequently denied read-only commands. Use when the user asks for a doctor run, checkup, audit, tune-up, or cleanup of their Claude Code setup or configuration.","argumentHint":"","aliases":["checkup"],"builtin":true},{"name":"loop","description":"Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Claude desktop app (also called the in-app browser, the browser pane, Claude's browser, or \"your own browser\"), whose tools are named mcp__Claude_Browser__* when the session runs in the desktop app and mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser__* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before those tools are turned on there may be a single enable__mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser tool instead. It covers the pane's persistent sign-ins, tabs and preview_start, reading pages as text, site approvals, what the pane cannot open, and what to do when it cannot be reached. It is not for Claude in Chrome (mcp__claude-in-chrome__* tools), which has its own skill, and it does not decide which browser to use. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["built-in-browser"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:chrome-browser","description":"Read this skill before the first step that uses Claude in Chrome, the browser extension whose tools are named mcp__claude-in-chrome__* (also called Chrome, the browser extension, or the external browser) and which acts in the person's real Chrome with their own sign-ins; before those tools are turned on there may be a single enable__mcp__claude-in-chrome tool instead. It covers loading the tools in one ToolSearch call, checking the person's open tabs and working in a new tab, site permissions, GIF recordings, console logs, dialogs to avoid, and when to stop and ask. It is not for the built-in browser (mcp__Claude_Browser__* or mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser__* tools), which has its own skill, and it does not decide which browser to use. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["chrome-browser"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:computer-use","description":"Read this skill before the first step of any request to do something in an app on the person's own computer (Notes, Finder, System Settings, any desktop app), to look at their screen, or for \"computer use\". Computer use (desktop control) lets Claude take screenshots of the person's desktop and control it with clicks, typing and scrolling through the Claude desktop app; its tools are named mcp__computer-use__* when the session runs in the desktop app and mcp__remote-devices__computer_* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before computer use is turned on for a conversation there may be no such tools, only an enable__mcp__remote-devices__computer tool, which turns it on. It covers turning it on, picking the right tool, the access flow, and the safety rules for tiered apps, links and financial actions. It is not for websites, which go through Claude in Chrome or the built-in browser and their own skills. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["computer-use"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:deep-research","description":"Use this skill when the user's prompt requires (1) researching a topic across multiple sources, comparing options or alternatives, analyzing trends or history, understanding markets or industries, or reviewing literature or studies and (2) synthesizing that research into a comprehensive, narrative report. If you're planning to search the web or internal knowledge bases, consider using this skill. This skill coordinates research subagents, so use it only when you have a tool for spawning subagents (the Agent or Task tool); otherwise, research the question directly. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":""},{"name":"anthropic-skills:docs","description":"docs (living docs people share, comment on and edit; use only when the user asks for one: names a doc, document, page, memo, spec, PRD, runbook or write-up, asks for somewhere to share or keep editing something, or says yes to your doc offer; a plan, comparison, summary or notes asked in chat stays in chat (at most a one-line doc offer); a report, status update, recap or \"something I can send them\" with no form named → ask first: reply, doc or file?; tabs hold tables and live charts too; a pasted claude.ai/code/artifact/… link may be a doc: check with docs tools first; not HTML pages, apps or plain chat answers; a .docx/.pptx/.xlsx/PDF asked for by name → that format's skill): asked for one → no docs-connector instructions in context? call the docs connector's `guide` with topic.instructions first, then create the doc (headings only, no body) before any search, file read or plan, even with files attached. Documenting code means docstrings or repo docs, not a doc. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["docs"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:docx","description":"Use this skill whenever the user wants to create, read, edit, or manipulate Word documents (.docx) or Word templates (.dotx). Triggers include: any mention of 'Word doc', 'word document', '.docx', '.dotx', or requests to produce professional documents with formatting like tables of contents, page numbers, or letterheads. Also use when extracting or reorganizing content from .docx or .dotx files, inserting or replacing images in documents, find-and-replace in Word files, working with tracked changes or comments, or converting content into a polished Word document. If the user asks for a 'report', 'memo', 'letter', 'template', or similar deliverable as a Word or .docx file (to download, email or print), use this skill. However, if they ask for a document, page, report, memo, or notes WITHOUT naming a file format and the session offers Claude's own dedicated document or page skill or connector, use that instead. Do NOT use for PDFs, spreadsheets, Google Docs, or coding unrelated to document generation. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["docx"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:import-memory","description":"Import a memory export from another AI assistant into Claude's memory — conversationally, additively, and with the content treated as data. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["import-memory"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:morning","description":"Render the user's morning brief as a styled HTML artifact, or set it up as a recurring weekday task. Use only when the user explicitly asks to run, see, or set up their morning brief, or if they invoke /morning by name. A question about their day, schedule, or calendar is not by itself a request for the brief; answer it directly instead. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["morning"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:pdf","description":"Use this skill whenever the user wants to do anything with PDF files. This includes reading or extracting text/tables from PDFs, combining or merging multiple PDFs into one, splitting PDFs apart, rotating pages, adding watermarks, creating new PDFs, filling PDF forms, encrypting/decrypting PDFs, extracting images, and OCR on scanned PDFs to make them searchable. If the user mentions a .pdf file or asks to produce one, use this skill. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["pdf"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:pptx","description":"Use this skill any time a .pptx or .potx file is involved in any way — as input, output, or both. This includes: creating slide decks, pitch decks, or presentations as PowerPoint (.pptx) files; reading, parsing, or extracting text from any .pptx or .potx file (even if the extracted content will be used elsewhere, like in an email, summary, or creating a different type of slide deck); editing, modifying, or updating existing presentations; combining or splitting slide files; working with templates (.potx), layouts, speaker notes, or comments. Trigger whenever the user asks for a PowerPoint or .pptx file, or references a .pptx or .potx filename, regardless of what they plan to do with the content afterward. However, when the user asks for a deck, slides, a slide deck, or a presentation without naming a file format, default to using a dedicated slide-deck artifact type or a separate slides skill if this session offers one; otherwise, use this skill. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["pptx"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:setup-writing-style","description":"Learns how the user writes from their own sent messages and docs, and builds a voice profile so future drafts sound like them instead of generic AI. The profile is saved as the my-writing-style skill. Use when the user asks to set up, learn, or capture their writing voice, or complains that drafts sound generic or unlike them and no my-writing-style profile exists. Only for drafting text the user will send as themselves, not for Claude's own replies. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["setup-writing-style"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:skill-creator","description":"Create new skills, modify and improve existing skills, and measure skill performance. Use when users want to create a skill from scratch, edit, or optimize an existing skill, run evals to test a skill, benchmark skill performance with variance analysis, or optimize a skill's description for better triggering accuracy. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["skill-creator"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:xlsx","description":"Use this skill any time a spreadsheet file is the primary input or output. This means any task where the user wants to: open, read, edit, or fix an existing .xlsx, .xlsm, .xltx, .csv, or .tsv file (e.g., adding columns, computing formulas, formatting, charting, cleaning messy data); create a new spreadsheet from scratch or from other data sources; or convert between tabular file formats. Trigger especially when the user references a spreadsheet file by name or path — even casually (like \"the xlsx in my downloads\") — and wants something done to it or produced from it. Also trigger for cleaning or restructuring messy tabular data files (malformed rows, misplaced headers, junk data) into proper spreadsheets. The deliverable must be a spreadsheet file. Do NOT trigger when the primary deliverable is a Word document, HTML report, standalone Python script, database pipeline, or Google Sheets API integration, even if tabular data is involved. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["xlsx"]}],"uuid":"80f4a36c-b4a6-4c9c-8cb6-678db336dccc","session_id":"311d41d8-3460-48c5-bd56-d1e1205b9591"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KTi411DVWfuUji2BrpyFKf","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * HrFinanceResolver — the HR finance tab's GraphQL surface.\n3\t *\n4\t * Financial data — role-gated to MODULE_MANAGER + TENANT_ADMIN (same\n5\t * shape as the payroll queries on HRResolver). Settings + category\n6\t * archival mutations are TENANT_ADMIN only.\n7\t *\n8\t * The Personnel Table, Personnel Salary and Labour Cost surfaces are all\n9\t * projections of the single `hrLabourCost` snapshot — the frontend reads\n10\t * it once and renders three tables from it, so the numbers never drift\n11\t * between tabs.\n12\t */\n13\timport { UnauthorizedException, UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';\n14\timport { Args, Context, ID, Int, Mutation, Query, Resolver } from '@nestjs/graphql';\n15\timport { CommandBus, QueryBus } from '@nestjs/cqrs';\n16\t\n17\timport {\n18\t  AuditLog,\n19\t  RequireTenantPermission,\n20\t  Role,\n21\t  Roles,\n22\t  hasResourcePermission,\n23\t  type ResourcePermissionUser,\n24\t} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/decorators';\n25\timport { RolesGuard } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/guards';\n26\t\n27\timport { GqlAuthGuard } from '../../common/guards/gql-auth.guard';\n28\timport {\n29\t  ArchiveHrFinanceCategoryCommand,\n30\t  CreateHrFinanceCategoryCommand,\n31\t  CreateHrFinanceEntryCommand,\n32\t  DeleteHrFinanceEntryCommand,\n33\t  RestoreHrFinanceCategoryCommand,\n34\t  UpdateHrFinanceCategoryCommand,\n35\t  UpdateHrFinanceEntryCommand,\n36\t  UpdatePayrollCostSettingsCommand,\n37\t} from '../commands/hr-finance.commands';\n38\timport {\n39\t  CreateHrFinanceCategoryInput,\n40\t  CreateHrFinanceEntryInput,\n41\t  UpdateHrFinanceCategoryInput,\n42\t  UpdateHrFinanceEntryInput,\n43\t  UpdatePayrollCostSettingsInput,\n44\t} from '../dto/hr-finance-inputs.dto';\n45\timport { HrFinanceSummary, HrLabourCost, HrPersonnelTable } from '../dto/hr-finance-outputs.dto';\n46\timport { HrFinanceCategory } from '../entities/hr-finance-category.entity';\n47\timport { HrFinanceEntry } from '../entities/hr-finance-entry.entity';\n48\timport { PayrollCostSettings } from '../entities/payroll-cost-settings.entity';\n49\timport {\n50\t  GetHrFinanceCategoriesQuery,\n51\t  GetHrFinanceEntriesQuery,\n52\t  GetHrFinanceSummaryQuery,\n53\t  GetHrLabourCostQuery,\n54\t  GetHrPersonnelTableQuery,\n55\t  GetPayrollCostSettingsQuery,\n56\t} from '../queries/hr-finance.queries';\n57\timport { HrFinanceGranularity } from '../query-handlers/get-hr-finance-summary.handler';\n58\timport { withLabourCostDecimals, withSummaryDecimals } from './hr-finance-decimal.mapper';\n59\t\n60\tinterface GraphQLContext {\n61\t  req: { user?: ({ sub: string; tenantId: string } & ResourcePermissionUser) | undefined };\n62\t}\n63\t\n64\tconst MAX_ENTRY_PAGE = 200;\n65\t\n66\t/**\n67\t * Salary-visibility capability (HR-MEDIUM-005). SUPER_ADMIN / TENANT_ADMIN\n68\t * bypass; any other role sees salary only if the tenant admin granted this\n69\t * permission to it — the tenant decides who sees pay, not a hardcoded role.\n70\t */\n71\tconst VIEW_SALARY_PERMISSION = 'hr_finance:view_salary';\n72\t\n73\t@UseGuards(GqlAuthGuard)\n74\t@Resolver(() => HrFinanceEntry)\n75\texport class HrFinanceResolver {\n76\t  constructor(\n77\t    private readonly commandBus: CommandBus,\n78\t    private readonly queryBus: QueryBus,\n79\t  ) {}\n80\t\n81\t  private getTenantId(context: GraphQLContext): string {\n82\t    const tenantId = context.req.user?.tenantId;\n83\t    if (!tenantId) {\n84\t      throw new UnauthorizedException('Tenant ID is required - authentication required');\n85\t    }\n86\t    return tenantId;\n87\t  }\n88\t\n89\t  private getUserId(context: GraphQLContext): string {\n90\t    const userId = context.req.user?.sub;\n91\t    if (!userId) {\n92\t      throw new UnauthorizedException('User ID is required - authentication required');\n93\t    }\n94\t    return userId;\n95\t  }\n96\t\n97\t  // ==========================================================================\n98\t  // Queries\n99\t  // ==========================================================================\n100\t\n101\t  /**\n102\t   * Headcount-only workforce projection — MANAGER + ADMIN, no salary permission\n103\t   * needed. The Personnel Table renders from this so a manager without\n104\t   * `hr_finance:view_salary` still sees headcounts (HR-MEDIUM-005).\n105\t   */\n106\t  @Query(() => HrPersonnelTable, { name: 'hrPersonnelTable' })\n107\t  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n108\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n109\t  async hrPersonnelTable(@Context() ctx: GraphQLContext): Promise<HrPersonnelTable> {\n110\t    return this.queryBus.execute(new GetHrPersonnelTableQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx)));\n111\t  }\n112\t\n113\t  /**\n114\t   * Salary-bearing labour-cost snapshot. Gated by `hr_finance:view_salary`\n115\t   * (TenantPermissionGuard) so a MODULE_MANAGER reaches it only when the tenant\n116\t   * admin granted the capability; TENANT_ADMIN / SUPER_ADMIN bypass.\n117\t   */\n118\t  @Query(() => HrLabourCost, { name: 'hrLabourCost' })\n119\t  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n120\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n121\t  @RequireTenantPermission(VIEW_SALARY_PERMISSION)\n122\t  async hrLabourCost(\n123\t    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n124\t    @Args('year', { type: () => Int, nullable: true }) year?: number,\n125\t  ): Promise<HrLabourCost> {\n126\t    const resolvedYear = year ?? new Date().getUTCFullYear();\n127\t    const result = await this.queryBus.execute<GetHrLabourCostQuery, HrLabourCost>(\n128\t      new GetHrLabourCostQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx), resolvedYear),\n129\t    );\n130\t    return withLabourCostDecimals(result);\n131\t  }\n132\t\n133\t  @Query(() => HrFinanceSummary, { name: 'hrFinanceSummary' })\n134\t  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n135\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n136\t  async hrFinanceSummary(\n137\t    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n138\t    @Args('from') from: Date,\n139\t    @Args('to') to: Date,\n140\t    @Args('granularity', {\n141\t      type: () => HrFinanceGranularity,\n142\t      defaultValue: HrFinanceGranularity.MONTH,\n143\t    })\n144\t    granularity: HrFinanceGranularity,\n145\t  ): Promise<HrFinanceSummary> {\n146\t    // Expenses + series are MANAGER-visible; per-department SALARY is withheld\n147\t    // unless the caller holds `hr_finance:view_salary` (HR-MEDIUM-005).\n148\t    const includeSalary = hasResourcePermission(ctx.req.user, VIEW_SALARY_PERMISSION);\n149\t    const result = await this.queryBus.execute<GetHrFinanceSummaryQuery, HrFinanceSummary>(\n150\t      new GetHrFinanceSummaryQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx), from, to, granularity, includeSalary),\n151\t    );\n152\t    return withSummaryDecimals(result);\n153\t  }\n154\t\n155\t  @Query(() => [HrFinanceCategory], { name: 'hrFinanceCategories' })\n156\t  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n157\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n158\t  async hrFinanceCategories(\n159\t    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n160\t    @Args('includeArchived', { defaultValue: false }) includeArchived: boolean,\n161\t  ): Promise<HrFinanceCategory[]> {\n162\t    return this.queryBus.execute(\n163\t      new GetHrFinanceCategoriesQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx), includeArchived),\n164\t    );\n165\t  }\n166\t\n167\t  @Query(() => [HrFinanceEntry], { name: 'hrFinanceEntries' })\n168\t  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n169\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n170\t  async hrFinanceEntries(\n171\t    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n172\t    @Args('from', { nullable: true }) from?: Date,\n173\t    @Args('to', { nullable: true }) to?: Date,\n174\t    @Args('categoryId', { type: () => ID, nullable: true }) categoryId?: string,\n175\t    @Args('departmentHrId', { type: () => ID, nullable: true }) departmentHrId?: string,\n176\t    @Args('limit', { type: () => Int, defaultValue: 50 }) limit?: number,\n177\t    @Args('offset', { type: () => Int, defaultValue: 0 }) offset?: number,\n178\t  ): Promise<HrFinanceEntry[]> {\n179\t    return this.queryBus.execute(\n180\t      new GetHrFinanceEntriesQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx), {\n181\t        from,\n182\t        to,\n183\t        categoryId,\n184\t        departmentHrId,\n185\t        limit: Math.min(Math.max(limit ?? 50, 1), MAX_ENTRY_PAGE),\n186\t        offset: Math.max(offset ?? 0, 0),\n187\t      }),\n188\t    );\n189\t  }\n190\t\n191\t  @Query(() => PayrollCostSettings, { name: 'payrollCostSettings' })\n192\t  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n193\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n194\t  async payrollCostSettings(@Context() ctx: GraphQLContext): Promise<PayrollCostSettings> {\n195\t    return this.queryBus.execute(new GetPayrollCostSettingsQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx)));\n196\t  }\n197\t\n198\t  // ==========================================================================\n199\t  // Mutations\n200\t  // ==========================================================================\n201\t\n202\t  @Mutation(() => HrFinanceEntry)\n203\t  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n204\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n205\t  @AuditLog({ action: 'CREATE_HR_FINANCE_ENTRY', resource: 'HrFinanceEntry', description: 'Book an HR finance entry' })\n206\t  async createHrFinanceEntry(\n207\t    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n208\t    @Args('input') input: CreateHrFinanceEntryInput,\n209\t  ): Promise<HrFinanceEntry> {\n210\t    return this.commandBus.execute(\n211\t      new CreateHrFinanceEntryCommand(this.getTenantId(ctx), input, this.getUserId(ctx)),\n212\t    );\n213\t  }\n214\t\n215\t  @Mutation(() => HrFinanceEntry)\n216\t  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n217\t  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n218\t  @AuditLog({ action: 'UPDATE_HR_FINANCE_ENTRY', resource: @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n232\t  @AuditLog({ action: 'DELETE_HR_FINANCE_ENTRY', resource: 'HrFinanceEntry', description: 'Soft-delete an HR finance entry' })\n233\t  async * HrFinanceResolver — the HR finance tab's GraphQL surface.\n *\n * Financial data — role-gated to MODULE_MANAGER + TENANT_ADMIN (same\n * shape as the payroll queries on HRResolver). Settings + category\n * archival mutations are TENANT_ADMIN only.\n *\n * The Personnel Table, Personnel Salary and Labour Cost surfaces are all\n * projections of the single `hrLabourCost` snapshot — the frontend reads\n * it once and renders three tables from it, so the numbers never drift\n * between tabs.\n */\nimport { UnauthorizedException, UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Args, Context, ID, Int, Mutation, Query, Resolver } from '@nestjs/graphql';\nimport { CommandBus, QueryBus } from '@nestjs/cqrs';\n\nimport {\n  AuditLog,\n  RequireTenantPermission,\n  Role,\n  Roles,\n  hasResourcePermission,\n  type ResourcePermissionUser,\n} from '@aquaculture/backend-common/decorators';\nimport { RolesGuard } from '@aquaculture/backend-common/guards';\n\nimport { GqlAuthGuard } from '../../common/guards/gql-auth.guard';\nimport {\n  ArchiveHrFinanceCategoryCommand,\n  CreateHrFinanceCategoryCommand,\n  CreateHrFinanceEntryCommand,\n  DeleteHrFinanceEntryCommand,\n  RestoreHrFinanceCategoryCommand,\n  UpdateHrFinanceCategoryCommand,\n  UpdateHrFinanceEntryCommand,\n  UpdatePayrollCostSettingsCommand,\n} from '../commands/hr-finance.commands';\nimport {\n  CreateHrFinanceCategoryInput,\n  CreateHrFinanceEntryInput,\n  UpdateHrFinanceCategoryInput,\n  UpdateHrFinanceEntryInput,\n  UpdatePayrollCostSettingsInput,\n} from '../dto/hr-finance-inputs.dto';\nimport { HrFinanceSummary, HrLabourCost, HrPersonnelTable } from '../dto/hr-finance-outputs.dto';\nimport { HrFinanceCategory } from '../entities/hr-finance-category.entity';\nimport { HrFinanceEntry } from '../entities/hr-finance-entry.entity';\nimport { PayrollCostSettings } from '../entities/payroll-cost-settings.entity';\nimport {\n  GetHrFinanceCategoriesQuery,\n  GetHrFinanceEntriesQuery,\n  GetHrFinanceSummaryQuery,\n  GetHrLabourCostQuery,\n  GetHrPersonnelTableQuery,\n  GetPayrollCostSettingsQuery,\n} from '../queries/hr-finance.queries';\nimport { HrFinanceGranularity } from '../query-handlers/get-hr-finance-summary.handler';\nimport { withLabourCostDecimals, withSummaryDecimals } from './hr-finance-decimal.mapper';\n\ninterface GraphQLContext {\n  req: { user?: ({ sub: string; tenantId: string } & ResourcePermissionUser) | undefined };\n}\n\nconst MAX_ENTRY_PAGE = 200;\n\n/**\n * Salary-visibility capability (HR-MEDIUM-005). SUPER_ADMIN / TENANT_ADMIN\n * bypass; any other role sees salary only if the tenant admin granted this\n * permission to it — the tenant decides who sees pay, not a hardcoded role.\n */\nconst VIEW_SALARY_PERMISSION = 'hr_finance:view_salary';\n\n@UseGuards(GqlAuthGuard)\n@Resolver(() => HrFinanceEntry)\nexport class HrFinanceResolver {\n  constructor(\n    private readonly commandBus: CommandBus,\n    private readonly queryBus: QueryBus,\n  ) {}\n\n  private getTenantId(context: GraphQLContext): string {\n    const tenantId = context.req.user?.tenantId;\n    if (!tenantId) {\n      throw new UnauthorizedException('Tenant ID is required - authentication required');\n    }\n    return tenantId;\n  }\n\n  private getUserId(context: GraphQLContext): string {\n    const userId = context.req.user?.sub;\n    if (!userId) {\n      throw new UnauthorizedException('User ID is required - authentication required');\n    }\n    return userId;\n  }\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Queries\n  // ==========================================================================\n\n  /**\n   * Headcount-only workforce projection — MANAGER + ADMIN, no salary permission\n   * needed. The Personnel Table renders from this so a manager without\n   * `hr_finance:view_salary` still sees headcounts (HR-MEDIUM-005).\n   */\n  @Query(() => HrPersonnelTable, { name: 'hrPersonnelTable' })\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  async hrPersonnelTable(@Context() ctx: GraphQLContext): Promise<HrPersonnelTable> {\n    return this.queryBus.execute(new GetHrPersonnelTableQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx)));\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Salary-bearing labour-cost snapshot. Gated by `hr_finance:view_salary`\n   * (TenantPermissionGuard) so a MODULE_MANAGER reaches it only when the tenant\n   * admin granted the capability; TENANT_ADMIN / SUPER_ADMIN bypass.\n   */\n  @Query(() => HrLabourCost, { name: 'hrLabourCost' })\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  @RequireTenantPermission(VIEW_SALARY_PERMISSION)\n  async hrLabourCost(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('year', { type: () => Int, nullable: true }) year?: number,\n  ): Promise<HrLabourCost> {\n    const resolvedYear = year ?? new Date().getUTCFullYear();\n    const result = await this.queryBus.execute<GetHrLabourCostQuery, HrLabourCost>(\n      new GetHrLabourCostQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx), resolvedYear),\n    );\n    return withLabourCostDecimals(result);\n  }\n\n  @Query(() => HrFinanceSummary, { name: 'hrFinanceSummary' })\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  async hrFinanceSummary(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('from') from: Date,\n    @Args('to') to: Date,\n    @Args('granularity', {\n      type: () => HrFinanceGranularity,\n      defaultValue: HrFinanceGranularity.MONTH,\n    })\n    granularity: HrFinanceGranularity,\n  ): Promise<HrFinanceSummary> {\n    // Expenses + series are MANAGER-visible; per-department SALARY is withheld\n    // unless the caller holds `hr_finance:view_salary` (HR-MEDIUM-005).\n    const includeSalary = hasResourcePermission(ctx.req.user, VIEW_SALARY_PERMISSION);\n    const result = await this.queryBus.execute<GetHrFinanceSummaryQuery, HrFinanceSummary>(\n      new GetHrFinanceSummaryQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx), from, to, granularity, includeSalary),\n    );\n    return withSummaryDecimals(result);\n  }\n\n  @Query(() => [HrFinanceCategory], { name: 'hrFinanceCategories' })\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  async hrFinanceCategories(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('includeArchived', { defaultValue: false }) includeArchived: boolean,\n  ): Promise<HrFinanceCategory[]> {\n    return this.queryBus.execute(\n      new GetHrFinanceCategoriesQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx), includeArchived),\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Query(() => [HrFinanceEntry], { name: 'hrFinanceEntries' })\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  async hrFinanceEntries(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('from', { nullable: true }) from?: Date,\n    @Args('to', { nullable: true }) to?: Date,\n    @Args('categoryId', { type: () => ID, nullable: true }) categoryId?: string,\n    @Args('departmentHrId', { type: () => ID, nullable: true }) departmentHrId?: string,\n    @Args('limit', { type: () => Int, defaultValue: 50 }) limit?: number,\n    @Args('offset', { type: () => Int, defaultValue: 0 }) offset?: number,\n  ): Promise<HrFinanceEntry[]> {\n    return this.queryBus.execute(\n      new GetHrFinanceEntriesQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx), {\n        from,\n        to,\n        categoryId,\n        departmentHrId,\n        limit: Math.min(Math.max(limit ?? 50, 1), MAX_ENTRY_PAGE),\n        offset: Math.max(offset ?? 0, 0),\n      }),\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Query(() => PayrollCostSettings, { name: 'payrollCostSettings' })\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  async payrollCostSettings(@Context() ctx: GraphQLContext): Promise<PayrollCostSettings> {\n    return this.queryBus.execute(new GetPayrollCostSettingsQuery(this.getTenantId(ctx)));\n  }\n\n  // ==========================================================================\n  // Mutations\n  // ==========================================================================\n\n  @Mutation(() => HrFinanceEntry)\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  @AuditLog({ action: 'CREATE_HR_FINANCE_ENTRY', resource: 'HrFinanceEntry', description: 'Book an HR finance entry' })\n  async createHrFinanceEntry(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('input') input: CreateHrFinanceEntryInput,\n  ): Promise<HrFinanceEntry> {\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new CreateHrFinanceEntryCommand(this.getTenantId(ctx), input, this.getUserId(ctx)),\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Mutation(() => HrFinanceEntry)\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  @AuditLog({ action: 'UPDATE_HR_FINANCE_ENTRY', resource: 'HrFinanceEntry', description: 'Update an HR finance entry' })\n  async updateHrFinanceEntry(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('id', { type: () => ID }) id: string,\n    @Args('input') input: UpdateHrFinanceEntryInput,\n  ): Promise<HrFinanceEntry> {\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new UpdateHrFinanceEntryCommand(this.getTenantId(ctx), id, input, this.getUserId(ctx)),\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Mutation(() => Boolean)\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  @AuditLog({ action: 'DELETE_HR_FINANCE_ENTRY', resource: 'HrFinanceEntry', description: 'Soft-delete an HR finance entry' })\n  async deleteHrFinanceEntry(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('id', { type: () => ID }) id: string,\n  ): Promise<boolean> {\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new DeleteHrFinanceEntryCommand(this.getTenantId(ctx), id, this.getUserId(ctx)),\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Mutation(() => HrFinanceCategory)\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  @AuditLog({ action: 'CREATE_HR_FINANCE_CATEGORY', resource: 'HrFinanceCategory', description: 'Create an HR finance category' })\n  async createHrFinanceCategory(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('input') input: CreateHrFinanceCategoryInput,\n  ): Promise<HrFinanceCategory> {\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new CreateHrFinanceCategoryCommand(this.getTenantId(ctx), input, this.getUserId(ctx)),\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Mutation(() => HrFinanceCategory)\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN, Role.MODULE_MANAGER)\n  @AuditLog({ action: 'UPDATE_HR_FINANCE_CATEGORY', resource: 'HrFinanceCategory', description: 'Update an HR finance category' })\n  async updateHrFinanceCategory(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('id', { type: () => ID }) id: string,\n    @Args('input') input: UpdateHrFinanceCategoryInput,\n  ): Promise<HrFinanceCategory> {\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new UpdateHrFinanceCategoryCommand(this.getTenantId(ctx), id, input, this.getUserId(ctx)),\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Mutation(() => HrFinanceCategory)\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN)\n  @AuditLog({ action: 'ARCHIVE_HR_FINANCE_CATEGORY', resource: 'HrFinanceCategory', description: 'Archive an HR finance category' })\n  async archiveHrFinanceCategory(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('id', { type: () => ID }) id: string,\n  ): Promise<HrFinanceCategory> {\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new ArchiveHrFinanceCategoryCommand(this.getTenantId(ctx), id, this.getUserId(ctx)),\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Mutation(() => HrFinanceCategory)\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN)\n  @AuditLog({ action: 'RESTORE_HR_FINANCE_CATEGORY', resource: 'HrFinanceCategory', description: 'Restore an HR finance category' })\n  async restoreHrFinanceCategory(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('id', { type: () => ID }) id: string,\n  ): Promise<HrFinanceCategory> {\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new RestoreHrFinanceCategoryCommand(this.getTenantId(ctx), id, this.getUserId(ctx)),\n    );\n  }\n\n  @Mutation(() => PayrollCostSettings)\n  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)\n  @Roles(Role.TENANT_ADMIN)\n  @AuditLog({ action: 'UPDATE_PAYROLL_COST_SETTINGS', resource: 'PayrollCostSettings', description: 'Update payroll cost settings' })\n  async updatePayrollCostSettings(\n    @Context() ctx: GraphQLContext,\n    @Args('input') input: UpdatePayrollCostSettingsInput,\n  ): Promise<PayrollCostSettings> {\n    return this.commandBus.execute(\n      new UpdatePayrollCostSettingsCommand(this.getTenantId(ctx), input, this.getUserId(ctx)),\n    );\n  }\n}\n","numLines":308,"startLine":1,"totalLines":308}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CfD4Ffsk9yEduS6eD12D1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_015pgGREdS2WiAzYRrnbmmb4","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-9d053dfeea6c/tests/invariants/hr-graphql-fe-be-parity.spec.ts","offset":55,"limit":45},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2671,"cache_read_input_tokens":70766,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2671},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"311d41d8-3460-48c5-bd56-d1e1205b9591","uuid":"50169579-9f4b-41fe-a0a7-a42d2ab1a2b5","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:45:19.174Z","request_id":"req_011CfD4FfJGA4fbBibfXfETR","wire_tool_inputs":{"toolu_015pgGREdS2WiAzYRrnbmmb4":{"file_path":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-9d053dfeea6c/tests/invariants/hr-graphql-fe-be-parity.spec.ts","offset":55,"limit":45}}}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015pgGREdS2WiAzYRrnbmmb4","type":"tool_result","content":"55\t  for (const file of listFiles(FE_SOURCE_ROOT, ['**/*.ts', '**/*.tsx'])) {\n56\t    if (file.includes('.spec.') || file.includes('.test.') || file.includes('test-setup')) continue;\n57\t    const src = readFileSync(join(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8');\n58\t    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;\n59\t    while ((match = re.exec(src)) !== null) {\n60\t      roots.push({ operation: match[1]!, field: match[2]!, file });\n61\t    }\n62\t  }\n63\t  return roots;\n64\t}\n65\t\n66\tdescribe('hr-module ↔ hr-service GraphQL parity', () => {\n67\t  const backendFields = extractBackendFieldSet(HR_BE_SOURCE_ROOT);\n68\t  const frontendRoots = extractFrontendRootFields();\n69\t\n70\t  it('backend extraction finds a plausible resolver surface (guards against silent extractor rot)', () => {\n71\t    // hr-service serves employee/payroll/leave/scheduling/training/finance\n72\t    // fields — dozens today. A collapse of the extractor to near-zero would make\n73\t    // the parity assertion below pass vacuously.\n74\t    expect(backendFields.size).toBeGreaterThan(30);\n75\t    // Canary fields spanning domains that must always exist.\n76\t    for (const canary of ['employees', 'payrolls', 'createEmployee', 'hrLabourCost']) {\n77\t      expect(backendFields).toContain(canary);\n78\t    }\n79\t  });\n80\t\n81\t  it('frontend extraction finds a plausible operation surface', () => {\n82\t    expect(frontendRoots.length).toBeGreaterThan(20);\n83\t  });\n84\t\n85\t  it('every frontend root field resolves to an hr-service resolver or an allowlisted federation field', () => {\n86\t    const unresolved = frontendRoots.filter(\n87\t      ({ field }) => !backendFields.has(field) && !(field in CROSS_SUBGRAPH_FIELDS),\n88\t    );\n89\t\n90\t    const report = unresolved\n91\t      .map(({ operation, field, file }) => `  ${operation} { ${field} } ← ${file}`)\n92\t      .join('\\n');\n93\t\n94\t    expect(\n95\t      unresolved.length === 0\n96\t        ? ''\n97\t        : `\\n${unresolved.length} frontend operation(s) target fields the hr subgraph does not serve.\\n` +\n98\t            `Fix the field name, implement the resolver, or (for federation fields) extend CROSS_SUBGRAPH_FIELDS:\\n${report}\\n`,\n99\t    ).toBe('');"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"311d41d8-3460-48c5-bd56-d1e1205b9591","uuid":"56657935-46ce-417d-bfd2-f50e408eb4d7","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:45:20.019Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-9d053dfeea6c/tests/invariants/hr-graphql-fe-be-parity.spec.ts","content":"  for (const file of listFiles(FE_SOURCE_ROOT, ['**/*.ts', '**/*.tsx'])) {\n    if (file.includes('.spec.') || file.includes('.test.') || file.includes('test-setup')) continue;\n    const src = readFileSync(join(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8');\n    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;\n    while ((match = re.exec(src)) !== null) {\n      roots.push({ operation: match[1]!, field: match[2]!, file });\n    }\n  }\n  return roots;\n}\n\ndescribe('hr-module ↔ hr-service GraphQL parity', () => {\n  const backendFields = extractBackendFieldSet(HR_BE_SOURCE_ROOT);\n  const frontendRoots = extractFrontendRootFields();\n\n  it('backend extraction finds a plausible resolver surface (guards against silent extractor rot)', () => {\n    // hr-service serves employee/payroll/leave/scheduling/training/finance\n    // fields — dozens today. A collapse of the extractor to near-zero would make\n    // the parity assertion below pass vacuously.\n    expect(backendFields.size).toBeGreaterThan(30);\n    // Canary fields spanning domains that must always exist.\n    for (const canary of ['employees', 'payrolls', 'createEmployee', 'hrLabourCost']) {\n      expect(backendFields).toContain(canary);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('frontend extraction finds a plausible operation surface', () => {\n    expect(frontendRoots.length).toBeGreaterThan(20);\n  });\n\n  it('every frontend root field resolves to an hr-service resolver or an allowlisted federation field', () => {\n    const unresolved = frontendRoots.filter(\n      ({ field }) => !backendFields.has(field) && !(field in CROSS_SUBGRAPH_FIELDS),\n    );\n\n    const report = unresolved\n      .map(({ operation, field, file }) => `  ${operation} { ${field} } ← ${file}`)\n      .join('\\n');\n\n    expect(\n      unresolved.length === 0\n        ? ''\n        : `\\n${unresolved.length} frontend operation(s) target fields the hr subgraph does not serve.\\n` +\n            `Fix the field name, implement the resolver, or (for federation fields) extend CROSS_SUBGRAPH_FIELDS:\\n${report}\\n`,\n    ).toBe('');","numLines":45,"startLine":55,"totalLines":102}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EBsevZ8XWeAMtwGzWBAESB","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Tenant-Permission-Guard Adoption Invariant\n3\t * ============================================================================\n4\t *\n5\t * Closes SENSOR-HIGH-022 (docs/reviews/2026-07-05-sensor-vfd-device-audit.md).\n6\t *\n7\t *   The `@RequireTenantPermission('<perm>')` decorator only enforces anything\n8\t *   when `TenantPermissionGuard` is active. The guard is opt-in (a handler\n9\t *   with no decorator passes through), so the safe wiring is a global\n10\t *   `APP_GUARD` registration in the owning service's `app.module.ts`.\n11\t *\n12\t *   Before this gate, sensor-service decorated its edge I/O-config mutations\n13\t *   with `@RequireTenantPermission('edge:manage-io-config')` but never\n14\t *   registered the guard — the fine-grained permission was dead metadata and\n15\t *   the weakest layer (the role gate) silently won.\n16\t *\n17\t * # What this spec enforces (Tier-3 \"make it detectable\")\n18\t *\n19\t *   For every `apps/<svc>` whose `src/**` uses `@RequireTenantPermission`,\n20\t *   that service's `app.module.ts` MUST register `TenantPermissionGuard` as an\n21\t *   `APP_GUARD`. Otherwise the decorator is inert.\n22\t *\n23\t * # When this spec fails\n24\t *\n25\t *   - A service added `@RequireTenantPermission` but did not wire the guard →\n26\t *     register `TenantPermissionGuard` as a fourth `APP_GUARD` (after\n27\t *     `RolesGuard`) in that service's `app.module.ts`.\n28\t */\n29\timport { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\n30\timport path from 'node:path';\n31\t\n32\tconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n33\tconst APPS_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'apps');\n34\t\n35\t/** Recursively collect *.ts files under a directory (skips node_modules/dist). */\n36\tfunction collectTsFiles(dir: string): string[] {\n37\t  const out: string[] = [];\n38\t  if (!existsSync(dir)) return out;\n39\t  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {\n40\t    if (entry === 'node_modules' || entry === 'dist') continue;\n41\t    const full = path.join(dir, entry);\n42\t    const st = statSync(full);\n43\t    if (st.isDirectory()) {\n44\t      out.push(...collectTsFiles(full));\n45\t    } else if (entry.endsWith('.ts') && !entry.endsWith('.spec.ts')) {\n46\t      out.push(full);\n47\t    }\n48\t  }\n49\t  return out;\n50\t}\n51\t\n52\tfunction servicesUsingDecorator(): string[] {\n53\t  const services: string[] = [];\n54\t  if (!existsSync(APPS_DIR)) return services;\n55\t  for (const svc of readdirSync(APPS_DIR)) {\n56\t    const srcDir = path.join(APPS_DIR, svc, 'src');\n57\t    if (!existsSync(srcDir)) continue;\n58\t    const uses = collectTsFiles(srcDir).some((f) =>\n59\t      /@RequireTenantPermission\\s*\\(/.test(readFileSync(f, 'utf8')),\n60\t    );\n61\t    if (uses) services.push(svc);\n62\t  }\n63\t  return services;\n64\t}\n65\t\n66\tdescribe('TenantPermissionGuard adoption (SENSOR-HIGH-022)', () => {\n67\t  const services = servicesUsingDecorator();\n68\t\n69\t  it('at least one service uses @RequireTenantPermission (sanity)', () => {\n70\t    // Guards against a false-green if the decorator is ever renamed/removed.\n71\t    expect(services.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n72\t  });\n73\t\n74\t  it.each(services)(\n75\t    '%s registers TenantPermissionGuard as an APP_GUARD',\n76\t    (svc) => {\n77\t      const modulePath = path.join(APPS_DIR, svc, 'src', 'app.module.ts');\n78\t      expect(existsSync(modulePath)).toBe(true);\n79\t      const src = readFileSync(modulePath, 'utf8');\n80\t      // The guard must be both referenced and wired to the APP_GUARD token.\n81\t      expect(src).toContain('TenantPermissionGuard');\n82\t      expect(/APP_GUARD[\\s\\S]{0,400}TenantPermissionGuard/.test(src)).toBe(true);\n83\t    },\n84\t  );\n85\t});\n86\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"311d41d8-3460-48c5-bd56-d1e1205b9591","uuid":"6a0182f3-82d2-457d-b98f-8845f9b4ba7b","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:45:20.823Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-9d053dfeea6c/tests/invariants/tenant-permission-guard-adoption.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Tenant-Permission-Guard Adoption Invariant\n * ============================================================================\n *\n * Closes SENSOR-HIGH-022 (docs/reviews/2026-07-05-sensor-vfd-device-audit.md).\n *\n *   The `@RequireTenantPermission('<perm>')` decorator only enforces anything\n *   when `TenantPermissionGuard` is active. The guard is opt-in (a handler\n *   with no decorator passes through), so the safe wiring is a global\n *   `APP_GUARD` registration in the owning service's `app.module.ts`.\n *\n *   Before this gate, sensor-service decorated its edge I/O-config mutations\n *   with `@RequireTenantPermission('edge:manage-io-config')` but never\n *   registered the guard — the fine-grained permission was dead metadata and\n *   the weakest layer (the role gate) silently won.\n *\n * # What this spec enforces (Tier-3 \"make it detectable\")\n *\n *   For every `apps/<svc>` whose `src/**` uses `@RequireTenantPermission`,\n *   that service's `app.module.ts` MUST register `TenantPermissionGuard` as an\n *   `APP_GUARD`. Otherwise the decorator is inert.\n *\n * # When this spec fails\n *\n *   - A service added `@RequireTenantPermission` but did not wire the guard →\n *     register `TenantPermissionGuard` as a fourth `APP_GUARD` (after\n *     `RolesGuard`) in that service's `app.module.ts`.\n */\nimport { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport path from 'node:path';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\nconst APPS_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'apps');\n\n/** Recursively collect *.ts files under a directory (skips node_modules/dist). */\nfunction collectTsFiles(dir: string): string[] {\n  const out: string[] = [];\n  if (!existsSync(dir)) return out;\n  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {\n    if (entry === 'node_modules' || entry === 'dist') continue;\n    const full = path.join(dir, entry);\n    const st = statSync(full);\n    if (st.isDirectory()) {\n      out.push(...collectTsFiles(full));\n    } else if (entry.endsWith('.ts') && !entry.endsWith('.spec.ts')) {\n      out.push(full);\n    }\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nfunction servicesUsingDecorator(): string[] {\n  const services: string[] = [];\n  if (!existsSync(APPS_DIR)) return services;\n  for (const svc of readdirSync(APPS_DIR)) {\n    const srcDir = path.join(APPS_DIR, svc, 'src');\n    if (!existsSync(srcDir)) continue;\n    const uses = collectTsFiles(srcDir).some((f) =>\n      /@RequireTenantPermission\\s*\\(/.test(readFileSync(f, 'utf8')),\n    );\n    if (uses) services.push(svc);\n  }\n  return services;\n}\n\ndescribe('TenantPermissionGuard adoption (SENSOR-HIGH-022)', () => {\n  const services = servicesUsingDecorator();\n\n  it('at least one service uses @RequireTenantPermission (sanity)', () => {\n    // Guards against a false-green if the decorator is ever renamed/removed.\n    expect(services.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n  });\n\n  it.each(services)(\n    '%s registers TenantPermissionGuard as an APP_GUARD',\n    (svc) => {\n      const modulePath = path.join(APPS_DIR, svc, 'src', 'app.module.ts');\n      expect(existsSync(modulePath)).toBe(true);\n      const src = readFileSync(modulePath, 'utf8');\n      // The guard must be both referenced and wired to the APP_GUARD token.\n      expect(src).toContain('TenantPermissionGuard');\n      expect(/APP_GUARD[\\s\\S]{0,400}TenantPermissionGuard/.test(src)).toBe(true);\n    },\n  );\n});\n","numLines":86,"startLine":1,"totalLines":86}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SzmxJmdn4NNjjyyN4FahTb","type":"tool_result","content":"455\t    if digest != recorded:\n456\t        return False, \"artifact_hash_mismatch\"\n457\t    return True, \"kernel_artifact_verified\"\n458\t\n459\t\n460\tdef _check_agent_ref(\n461\t    ref: str,\n462\t    *,\n463\t    root: Path,\n464\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n465\t    checked: list[str],\n466\t    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n467\t    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n468\t) -> None:\n469\t    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n470\t    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n471\t    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n472\t    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n473\t    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n474\t    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n475\t    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n476\t    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n477\t    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n478\t    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n479\t    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n480\t    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n481\t        checked.append(ref)\n482\t        return\n483\t    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n484\t        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n485\t        if admissible:\n486\t            checked.append(ref)\n487\t            return\n488\t        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n489\t            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n490\t            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n491\t            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n492\t            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n493\t            errors.append({\n494\t                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n495\t                \"ref\": ref,\n496\t                \"reason\": reason,\n497\t            })\n498\t            return\n499\t    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n500\t    if parsed is None:\n501\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n502\t        return\n503\t    path, line = parsed\n504\t    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n505\t    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n506\t    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n507\t    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n508\t    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n509\t    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n510\t    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n511\t    try:\n512\t        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n513\t    except _GE as exc:\n514\t        msg = str(exc)\n515\t        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n516\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n517\t        else:\n518\t            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n519\t                           \"error\": msg})\n520\t        return\n521\t    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n522\t    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n523\t    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n524\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n525\t        return\n526\t    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n527\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n528\t        return\n529\t    checked.append(rel_str)\n530\t    if line is None:\n531\t        return\n532\t    if line <= 0:\n533\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_invalid\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line})\n534\t        return\n535\t    line_count = len(absolute.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines())\n536\t    if line > line_count:\n537\t        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_missing\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line, \"line_count\": line_count})\n538\t\n539\t\n540\tdef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n541\t    *,\n542\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n543\t    workspace_root: str | Path,\n544\t    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n545\t    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n546\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n547\t    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n548\t\n549\t    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n550\t    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n551\t    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n552\t    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n553\t    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n554\t    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n555\t    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n556\t\n557\t    Returns: {\n558\t        \"valid\": bool,\n559\t        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],\n560\t        \"checked_refs\": [<sorted unique paths>],\n561\t    }\n562\t    \"\"\"\n563\t    root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()\n564\t    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"311d41d8-3460-48c5-bd56-d1e1205b9591","uuid":"e33c2bf5-4f2e-48ce-8925-f7b961dff622","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:46:07.276Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-9d053dfeea6c/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/evidence_validator.py","content":"    if digest != recorded:\n        return False, \"artifact_hash_mismatch\"\n    return True, \"kernel_artifact_verified\"\n\n\ndef _check_agent_ref(\n    ref: str,\n    *,\n    root: Path,\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    checked: list[str],\n    allow_self_output: bool = False,\n    allow_kernel_artifacts: bool = False,\n) -> None:\n    # Y7 follow-through (ORPHAN-708) — the kernel's OWN adjudication mint\n    # (human_required_adjudication.open_adjudication) issues\n    # ``human-required:<request-id>`` refs, and the first real panel drain\n    # showed this validator rejecting them: mint-side and law-side of the\n    # same kernel disagreed, every panel opinion died submit_rejected, and\n    # the rejection BURNED the envelope's requeue budget. The ref is a\n    # LEDGER POINTER, not a repo path — the load-bearing verification\n    # happens at fold time (fold_adjudication / adjudicate_human_required\n    # resolve the record from the store before any disposition acts), so\n    # the law admits the pointer as checked rather than pretending a repo\n    # check that cannot exist for state-store records.\n    if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n        checked.append(ref)\n        return\n    if allow_kernel_artifacts:\n        admissible, reason = _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)\n        if admissible:\n            checked.append(ref)\n            return\n        if reason != \"artifact_not_recorded_in_results_ledger\":\n            # It IS an artifact path — just not one the ledger vouches for.\n            # Naming the failure is the point: a silent fall-through to the\n            # repo check would report \"not repo verified\" about a file that\n            # was never meant to be in the repo.\n            errors.append({\n                \"code\": \"agent_evidence_artifact_unverifiable\",\n                \"ref\": ref,\n                \"reason\": reason,\n            })\n            return\n    parsed = _parse_agent_ref(ref)\n    if parsed is None:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_malformed\", \"ref\": ref})\n        return\n    path, line = parsed\n    # Plan 024 v3 §H-5 — canonical-resolve BEFORE the SELF_OUTPUT\n    # prefix check. Pre-fix the prefix match operated on the\n    # lexically-normalized string; a `src/../aria-tools/...` traversal\n    # could bypass detection if normalize_path didn't fully collapse\n    # it. Post-fix the resolution runs first, then SELF_OUTPUT is\n    # decided on the canonical posix-relative form.\n    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError as _GE\n    try:\n        rel_str, absolute = _canonical_evidence_path(path, root)\n    except _GE as exc:\n        msg = str(exc)\n        if msg.startswith(\"evidence_path_outside_repo\"):\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_escapes_workspace\", \"path\": path})\n        else:\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_unresolvable\", \"path\": path,\n                           \"error\": msg})\n        return\n    # ARIA self-output / runtime artefacts are NOT admissible evidence\n    # for an agent claim (CLAUDE.md L1, Plan 016 §Agent output untrusted).\n    if not allow_self_output and rel_str.startswith(SELF_OUTPUT_MARKERS):\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_self_output\", \"path\": rel_str})\n        return\n    if not absolute.exists() or not absolute.is_file():\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_path_missing\", \"path\": path})\n        return\n    checked.append(rel_str)\n    if line is None:\n        return\n    if line <= 0:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_invalid\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line})\n        return\n    line_count = len(absolute.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\").splitlines())\n    if line > line_count:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_line_missing\", \"path\": path, \"line\": line, \"line_count\": line_count})\n\n\ndef validate_agent_response_evidence(\n    *,\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    workspace_root: str | Path,\n    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    probe_session: GitProbeSession | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Re-fetch every evidence ref claimed by an agent response and verify it exists in the repo.\n\n    Why: Plan 016 V-24 — agent output is data, not truth. The contract\n    layer (agent_contract.validate_response) checks the SHAPE of the\n    satisfaction matrix and the response envelope. This function checks\n    that every evidence_ref the agent attached to its verdicts actually\n    points at a real file (and line, when given) at the workspace SHA.\n    A response that passes both gates is contractually safe to publish\n    as a CONVERGED plan / promoted finding.\n\n    Returns: {\n        \"valid\": bool,\n        \"errors\": [{\"code\": ..., \"path\": ..., ...}],\n        \"checked_refs\": [<sorted unique paths>],\n    }\n    \"\"\"\n    root = Path(workspace_root).resolve()\n    errors: list[dict[str, Any]] = []","numLines":110,"startLine":455,"totalLines":774}}}
