{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VABfrfxAnBm77bweGKQTam","type":"tool_result","content":"363\tdef validate_response(\n364\t    envelope: dict[str, Any],\n365\t    *,\n366\t    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n367\t    lease: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n368\t) -> None:\n369\t    \"\"\"Validate an `aria/agent-response/v1` envelope.\n370\t\n371\t    `request` is optional — when provided, the response is cross-checked\n372\t    against the request's `must_satisfy` ids and `expected_output_path`.\n373\t\n374\t    `lease` is optional — when provided (Plan 023 v3 §A-5), the\n375\t    envelope's claim_id and agent_id MUST match the leased identity.\n376\t    Pre-Plan-023 the envelope's claim_id was only validated for SHAPE\n377\t    (CLAIM_ID_RE pattern) and the request_id was matched to the\n378\t    request, but envelope.claim_id and envelope.agent_id were never\n379\t    bound to the lease that authorized the submission. A stitched\n380\t    envelope from a different lease (same request_id but different\n381\t    claim/agent) would pass.\n382\t    \"\"\"\n383\t    missing = [f for f in RESPONSE_REQUIRED_FIELDS if f not in envelope]\n384\t    if missing:\n385\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response missing required fields: {missing}\")\n386\t    if envelope[\"$schema\"] != RESPONSE_SCHEMA:\n387\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response $schema must be {RESPONSE_SCHEMA!r}\")\n388\t    if not isinstance(envelope[\"request_id\"], str) or not REQUEST_ID_RE.match(envelope[\"request_id\"]):\n389\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.request_id invalid: {envelope['request_id']!r}\")\n390\t    claim_id = envelope[\"claim_id\"]\n391\t    if not isinstance(claim_id, str) or not CLAIM_ID_RE.match(claim_id):\n392\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.claim_id invalid: {claim_id!r}\")\n393\t    agent_id = envelope[\"agent_id\"]\n394\t    if not isinstance(agent_id, str) or not AGENT_ID_RE.match(agent_id):\n395\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.agent_id invalid: {agent_id!r}\")\n396\t    role = envelope[\"role\"]\n397\t    if role not in INVOCATION_ROLES:\n398\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.role unknown: {role!r}\")\n399\t    status = envelope[\"status\"]\n400\t    if status not in RESPONSE_STATUSES:\n401\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.status unknown: {status!r}\")\n402\t\n403\t    matrix = envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"]\n404\t    if not isinstance(matrix, list):\n405\t        raise GovernanceError(\"agent-response.satisfaction_matrix must be a list\")\n406\t    matrix_entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n407\t    matrix_ids: set[str] = set()\n408\t    for idx, entry in enumerate(matrix):\n409\t        if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n410\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}] must be an object\")\n411\t        item_id = entry.get(\"id\")\n412\t        verdict = entry.get(\"verdict\")\n413\t        if not isinstance(item_id, str) or not item_id.strip():\n414\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id is required\")\n415\t        if item_id in matrix_ids:\n416\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id duplicate: {item_id}\")\n417\t        matrix_ids.add(item_id)\n418\t        if verdict not in SATISFACTION_VERDICTS:\n419\t            raise GovernanceError(\n420\t                f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].verdict {verdict!r} not in {SATISFACTION_VERDICTS}\"\n421\t            )\n422\t        if verdict in {\"blocked\", \"contradicted\"}:\n423\t            note = entry.get(\"note\") or entry.get(\"rationale\") or \"\"\n424\t            if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n425\t                raise GovernanceError(\n426\t                    f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].note required when verdict={verdict!r}\"\n427\t                )\n428\t            _check_banned_phrases(note, field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].note\")\n429\t            evidence_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\", [])\n430\t            _ensure_string_list(\n431\t                evidence_refs,\n432\t                field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].evidence_refs\",\n433\t                allow_empty=False,\n434\t            )\n435\t        matrix_entries.append(entry)\n436\t\n437\t    rationale = envelope.get(\"rationale\")\n438\t    if isinstance(rationale, str) and rationale.strip():\n439\t        _check_banned_phrases(rationale, field=\"agent-response.rationale\")\n440\t\n441\t    # Plan 023 v3 §A-5 — envelope claim/agent identity bound to lease.\n442\t    # When the caller supplies the leased claim+agent identity (which\n443\t    # submit_claim_result does), reject any envelope whose claim_id or\n444\t    # agent_id differs. Pre-fix a stitched envelope from a different\n445\t    # lease (same request_id but different claim/agent) passed because\n446\t    # only request_id was cross-checked.\n447\t    if lease is not None:\n448\t        leased_claim = lease.get(\"claim_id\")\n449\t        leased_agent = lease.get(\"agent_id\")\n450\t        if leased_claim is not None and envelope[\"claim_id\"] != leased_claim:\n451\t            raise GovernanceError(\n452\t                f\"envelope_claim_id_mismatch: envelope.claim_id=\"\n453\t                f\"{envelope['claim_id']!r} does not match leased claim_id=\"\n454\t                f\"{leased_claim!r}\"\n455\t            )\n456\t        if leased_agent is not None and envelope[\"agent_id\"] != leased_agent:\n457\t            raise GovernanceError(\n458\t                f\"envelope_agent_id_mismatch: envelope.agent_id=\"\n459\t                f\"{envelope['agent_id']!r} does not match leased agent_id=\"\n460\t                f\"{leased_agent!r}\"\n461\t            )\n462\t\n463\t    if request is not None:\n464\t        if envelope[\"request_id\"] != request.get(\"request_id\"):\n465\t            raise GovernanceError(\n466\t                f\"agent-response.request_id {envelope['request_id']!r} does not match request \"\n467\t                f\"{request.get('request_id')!r}\"\n468\t            )\n469\t        # Plan 024 v3 §H-4 — envelope.role must match request.role.\n470\t        # Pre-fix only the membership check above (line 322) ensured\n471\t        # the role was in REQUEST_ROLES; a request bound to role\n472\t        # `domain_review` could accept a response with role\n473\t        # `architectural_judgment` and pass cross-checks because the\n474\t        # equality with request.role was never asserted. The\n475\t        # downstream judgment_bridge + consensus path reads\n476\t        # response.role to route the verdict, so a mismatched role\n477\t        # silently misroutes the judgment.\n478\t        request_role = request.get(\"role\")\n479\t        if request_role is not None and envelope.get(\"role\") != request_role:\n480\t            raise GovernanceError(\n481\t                f\"response_role_mismatch_with_request: \"\n482\t                f\"envelope.role={envelope.get('role')!r}, \"\n483\t                f\"request.role={request_role!r}\"\n484\t            )\n485\t        # Every must_satisfy item must appear once in the matrix.\n486\t        required_ids = {item[\"id\"] for item in request.get(\"must_satisfy\", [])}\n487\t        unmet = required_ids - matrix_ids\n488\t        if unmet:\n489\t            raise GovernanceError(\n490\t                f\"satisfaction_matrix missing entries for must_satisfy ids: {sorted(unmet)}\"\n491\t            )\n492\t        extra = matrix_ids - required_ids\n493\t        if extra:\n494\t            raise GovernanceError(\n495\t                f\"satisfaction_matrix contains ids not in must_satisfy: {sorted(extra)}\"\n496\t            )\n497\t        if envelope.get(\"output_path\") and request.get(\"expected_output_path\"):\n498\t            if envelope[\"output_path\"] != request[\"expected_output_path\"]:\n499\t                raise GovernanceError(\n500\t                    f\"agent-response.output_path {envelope['output_path']!r} differs from \"\n501\t                    f\"request.expected_output_path {request['expected_output_path']!r}\"\n502\t                )"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"311d41d8-3460-48c5-bd56-d1e1205b9591","uuid":"82c86df7-fa61-45f7-af89-0e3da728202a","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:46:08.611Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-9d053dfeea6c/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/agent_contract.py","content":"def validate_response(\n    envelope: dict[str, Any],\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    lease: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Validate an `aria/agent-response/v1` envelope.\n\n    `request` is optional — when provided, the response is cross-checked\n    against the request's `must_satisfy` ids and `expected_output_path`.\n\n    `lease` is optional — when provided (Plan 023 v3 §A-5), the\n    envelope's claim_id and agent_id MUST match the leased identity.\n    Pre-Plan-023 the envelope's claim_id was only validated for SHAPE\n    (CLAIM_ID_RE pattern) and the request_id was matched to the\n    request, but envelope.claim_id and envelope.agent_id were never\n    bound to the lease that authorized the submission. A stitched\n    envelope from a different lease (same request_id but different\n    claim/agent) would pass.\n    \"\"\"\n    missing = [f for f in RESPONSE_REQUIRED_FIELDS if f not in envelope]\n    if missing:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response missing required fields: {missing}\")\n    if envelope[\"$schema\"] != RESPONSE_SCHEMA:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response $schema must be {RESPONSE_SCHEMA!r}\")\n    if not isinstance(envelope[\"request_id\"], str) or not REQUEST_ID_RE.match(envelope[\"request_id\"]):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.request_id invalid: {envelope['request_id']!r}\")\n    claim_id = envelope[\"claim_id\"]\n    if not isinstance(claim_id, str) or not CLAIM_ID_RE.match(claim_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.claim_id invalid: {claim_id!r}\")\n    agent_id = envelope[\"agent_id\"]\n    if not isinstance(agent_id, str) or not AGENT_ID_RE.match(agent_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.agent_id invalid: {agent_id!r}\")\n    role = envelope[\"role\"]\n    if role not in INVOCATION_ROLES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.role unknown: {role!r}\")\n    status = envelope[\"status\"]\n    if status not in RESPONSE_STATUSES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.status unknown: {status!r}\")\n\n    matrix = envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"]\n    if not isinstance(matrix, list):\n        raise GovernanceError(\"agent-response.satisfaction_matrix must be a list\")\n    matrix_entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    matrix_ids: set[str] = set()\n    for idx, entry in enumerate(matrix):\n        if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}] must be an object\")\n        item_id = entry.get(\"id\")\n        verdict = entry.get(\"verdict\")\n        if not isinstance(item_id, str) or not item_id.strip():\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id is required\")\n        if item_id in matrix_ids:\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id duplicate: {item_id}\")\n        matrix_ids.add(item_id)\n        if verdict not in SATISFACTION_VERDICTS:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].verdict {verdict!r} not in {SATISFACTION_VERDICTS}\"\n            )\n        if verdict in {\"blocked\", \"contradicted\"}:\n            note = entry.get(\"note\") or entry.get(\"rationale\") or \"\"\n            if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n                raise GovernanceError(\n                    f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].note required when verdict={verdict!r}\"\n                )\n            _check_banned_phrases(note, field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].note\")\n            evidence_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\", [])\n            _ensure_string_list(\n                evidence_refs,\n                field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].evidence_refs\",\n                allow_empty=False,\n            )\n        matrix_entries.append(entry)\n\n    rationale = envelope.get(\"rationale\")\n    if isinstance(rationale, str) and rationale.strip():\n        _check_banned_phrases(rationale, field=\"agent-response.rationale\")\n\n    # Plan 023 v3 §A-5 — envelope claim/agent identity bound to lease.\n    # When the caller supplies the leased claim+agent identity (which\n    # submit_claim_result does), reject any envelope whose claim_id or\n    # agent_id differs. Pre-fix a stitched envelope from a different\n    # lease (same request_id but different claim/agent) passed because\n    # only request_id was cross-checked.\n    if lease is not None:\n        leased_claim = lease.get(\"claim_id\")\n        leased_agent = lease.get(\"agent_id\")\n        if leased_claim is not None and envelope[\"claim_id\"] != leased_claim:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"envelope_claim_id_mismatch: envelope.claim_id=\"\n                f\"{envelope['claim_id']!r} does not match leased claim_id=\"\n                f\"{leased_claim!r}\"\n            )\n        if leased_agent is not None and envelope[\"agent_id\"] != leased_agent:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"envelope_agent_id_mismatch: envelope.agent_id=\"\n                f\"{envelope['agent_id']!r} does not match leased agent_id=\"\n                f\"{leased_agent!r}\"\n            )\n\n    if request is not None:\n        if envelope[\"request_id\"] != request.get(\"request_id\"):\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"agent-response.request_id {envelope['request_id']!r} does not match request \"\n                f\"{request.get('request_id')!r}\"\n            )\n        # Plan 024 v3 §H-4 — envelope.role must match request.role.\n        # Pre-fix only the membership check above (line 322) ensured\n        # the role was in REQUEST_ROLES; a request bound to role\n        # `domain_review` could accept a response with role\n        # `architectural_judgment` and pass cross-checks because the\n        # equality with request.role was never asserted. The\n        # downstream judgment_bridge + consensus path reads\n        # response.role to route the verdict, so a mismatched role\n        # silently misroutes the judgment.\n        request_role = request.get(\"role\")\n        if request_role is not None and envelope.get(\"role\") != request_role:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"response_role_mismatch_with_request: \"\n                f\"envelope.role={envelope.get('role')!r}, \"\n                f\"request.role={request_role!r}\"\n            )\n        # Every must_satisfy item must appear once in the matrix.\n        required_ids = {item[\"id\"] for item in request.get(\"must_satisfy\", [])}\n        unmet = required_ids - matrix_ids\n        if unmet:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"satisfaction_matrix missing entries for must_satisfy ids: {sorted(unmet)}\"\n            )\n        extra = matrix_ids - required_ids\n        if extra:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"satisfaction_matrix contains ids not in must_satisfy: {sorted(extra)}\"\n            )\n        if envelope.get(\"output_path\") and request.get(\"expected_output_path\"):\n            if envelope[\"output_path\"] != request[\"expected_output_path\"]:\n                raise GovernanceError(\n                    f\"agent-response.output_path {envelope['output_path']!r} differs from \"\n                    f\"request.expected_output_path {request['expected_output_path']!r}\"\n                )","numLines":140,"startLine":363,"totalLines":616}}}

ORPHAN-CRITICAL-734 — role-scoped, not global. Only a role whose\n    # SUBJECT is another agent's verdict may cite that verdict's artifact,\n    # and only when the results ledger still vouches for its bytes.\n    allow_kernel_artifacts = str(\n        (request or {}).get(\"role\") or \"\",\n    ) in ARBITRATION_ROLES\n    # ONE probe session for the whole submission (evidence_probe): the\n    # baseline is resolved once for all refs, and every git probe of this\n    # decision shares one liveness clock — the seam that used to hand each\n    # ref a 5 s budget and re-dispatched the whole paid run on one stall.\n    # The submit decision passes the session it already resolved its\n    # evidence target on, so a target git could not read is remembered here\n    # and every ref grades `verification_unavailable` rather than being\n    # compared against an anchor the submitter did not cite.\n    probe_session = probe_session if probe_session is not None else GitProbeSession()\n\n    response_refs = response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    if not isinstance(response_refs, list):\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_refs_not_list\"})\n        response_refs = []\n    for ref in response_refs:\n        if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_evidence_ref_not_string\"})\n            continue\n        _check_agent_ref(\n            ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n            allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n        )\n        if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n            continue  # Z2 — a ledger pointer has no repo classification\n        if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)[0]:\n            # Verified by content hash against the results ledger — the\n            # repo classification below cannot describe it (the artifact\n            # is state-store-owned and was never meant to be committed).\n            continue\n        envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n            ref,\n            workspace_root=root,\n            source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n            target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n            probe_session=probe_session,\n        )\n        evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n        try:\n            EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n        except GovernanceError as exc:\n            errors.append({\n                \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n                \"ref\": ref,\n                \"reason\": str(exc),\n            })\n\n    # Plan 024 §B-2 — satisfaction_matrix non-empty enforcement.\n    # Pre-fix an agent response with `satisfaction_matrix: []` passed\n    # consensus because the matrix-iteration loop simply never ran.\n    # Combined with the empty-allowed_scope skip (line below), a judge\n    # could rubber-stamp a request with zero criteria checked. The\n    # request can opt out via `allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix=True`\n    # for the rare judge-only domain that explicitly does not produce\n    # per-criterion evidence — operator-approval-gated; not a default.\n    matrix = response.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\") or []\n    allow_empty_matrix = bool(request and request.get(\"allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix\"))\n    if not matrix and not allow_empty_matrix:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty\"})\n    if isinstance(matrix, list):\n        for entry in matrix:\n            if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n                continue\n            entry_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n            if not isinstance(entry_refs, list):\n                errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_refs_not_list\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")})\n                continue\n            for ref in entry_refs:\n                if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n                    errors.append(\n                        {\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_ref_not_string\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")}\n                    )\n                    continue\n                _check_agent_ref(\n                    ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n                    allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n                )\n                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n                    continue  # Z2 — same single definition as above\n                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)[0]:\n                    continue  # ORPHAN-734 — same single definition as above\n                envelope = classify_evidence_ref(","numLines":90,"startLine":564,"totalLines":774}}}

Return whichever exists.\n67\t    \"\"\"\n68\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), dict):\n69\t        return dict(details[\"verdict\"])\n70\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"consensus\"), dict):\n71\t        return dict(details[\"consensus\"])\n72\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), str):\n73\t        # Flat shape (ORPHAN-HIGH-629 sibling): the judge contract file says\n74\t        # BOTH \"Return JSON with: verdict, judge_id, confidence...\" and\n75\t        # \"details.verdict.rationale\". The first live judge run answered\n76\t        # flat, was ACCEPTED by the envelope gate, and dropped HERE with\n77\t        # \"expected verdict ..., got None\" — a verdict lost between two\n78\t        # halves of one document. Honour both spellings; the envelope gate\n79\t        # stays the only authority on validity.\n80\t        return {\n81\t            key: details.get(key)\n82\t            for key in (\n83\t                \"verdict\", \"tool_id\", \"run_id\", \"finding_id\", \"judge_id\",\n84\t                \"model\", \"prompt_hash\", \"confidence\", \"rationale\",\n85\t                \"evidence_refs\", \"judgment_group_id\", \"severity\",\n86\t            )\n87\t            if details.get(key) is not None\n88\t        }\n89\t    return {}\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tdef validate_judge_response(\n93\t    *,\n94\t    request: dict[str, Any],\n95\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n96\t) -> list[str]:\n97\t    \"\"\"Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — the judge output contract, checkable BEFORE accept.\n98\t\n99\t    The second sealed night accepted 12 judge results whose envelopes never\n100\t    carried a verdict the bridge could read (\"expected verdict ..., got\n101\t    None\") — each one an accepted row that could never fold, burning bridge\n102\t    retries toward permanent_fail. This is the SINGLE definition of what a\n103\t    judge response must carry; ``record_judge_verdict_from_response`` raises\n104\t    on exactly this list, and the executor's pre-submit gate releases the\n105\t    claim on it instead of sealing an unfoldable result.\n106\t\n107\t    Returns [] for non-judge roles: the contract binds judges only.\n108\t    \"\"\"\n109\t    role = response.get(\"role\")\n110\t    if not is_judge_role(role):\n111\t        return []\n112\t    errors: list[str] = []\n113\t    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n114\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n115\t        details = {}\n116\t    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n117\t    if not verdict_block:\n118\t        return [\"judge_verdict:absent\"]\n119\t    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n120\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n121\t        # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's contract says \"return\n122\t        # details.consensus, OR the uncertainty reason when the consensus\n123\t        # gate cannot be met\", and the deterministic engine it mirrors\n124\t        # emits exactly that non-verdict outcome. The live arbiter obeyed\n125\t        # and this contract burned the claim as invalid:None — fourth\n126\t        # mint-vs-law instance. Uncertainty is a VALID arbitration\n127\t        # outcome, for the arbiter alone, with a reason from the closed\n128\t        # vocabulary; binary judges stay bound to binary verdicts.\n129\t        reason = verdict_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n130\t        if (\n131\t            role == \"consensus_arbitration\"\n132\t            and verdict is None\n133\t            and reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS\n134\t        ):\n135\t            pass\n136\t        else:\n137\t            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.verdict:invalid:{verdict!r}\")\n138\t    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n139\t    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n140\t    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n141\t    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n142\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_tool_run_or_finding_id\")\n143\t    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n144\t    if not judge_id:\n145\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_judge_identity\")\n146\t    elif str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n147\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:executor_shaped_judge_identity\")\n148\t    return errors\n149\t\n150\t\n151\tdef record_judge_verdict_from_response(\n152\t    *,\n153\t    request: dict[str, Any],\n154\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n155\t    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n156\t) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n157\t    \"\"\"Translate an ACCEPTED judge response into an ai_judge feedback row.\n158\t\n159\t    Returns the persisted row, or None when the envelope role is not a\n160\t    judge role (the bridge is a no-op for non-judge submissions).\n161\t\n162\t    Raises GovernanceError when the response is missing the data the\n163\t    consensus engine requires (tool_id / run_id / finding_id come from\n164\t    the request envelope's optional fields; verdict + severity from\n165\t    the response.details).\n166\t    \"\"\"\n167\t    role = response.get(\"role\")\n168\t    if not is_judge_role(role):\n169\t        return None\n170\t\n171\t    # Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — one contract, two consumers: the executor's\n172\t    # pre-submit gate checks exactly this list and releases instead of\n173\t    # sealing an unfoldable accepted result; the bridge raises on it so a\n174\t    # row that somehow slipped past still fails loudly, never half-folds.\n175\t    contract_errors = validate_judge_response(request=request, response=response)\n176\t    if contract_errors:\n177\t        raise GovernanceError(\n178\t            \"judge bridge contract violation: \" + \"; \".join(contract_errors)\n179\t        )\n180\t\n181\t    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n182\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n183\t        details = {}\n184\t\n185\t    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's legitimate non-verdict outcome\n186\t    # folds through the SAME producer, ledger and idempotent escalation_id\n187\t    # as the deterministic engine, so one sweep drains both lanes into one\n188\t    # HUMAN_REQUIRED record. It is NOT a feedback verdict: an uncertain\n189\t    # arbitration must never write an ai_judge row.\n190\t    if role == \"consensus_arbitration\":\n191\t        arb_block = _verdict_field(details)\n192\t        arb_reason = arb_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n193\t        if arb_block.get(\"verdict\") is None and arb_reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS:\n194\t            from .feedback_store import record_consensus_uncertainty\n195\t\n196\t            row = record_consensus_uncertainty(\n197\t                tool_id=str(request.get(\"tool_id\") or arb_block.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n198\t                run_id=str(request.get(\"run_id\") or arb_block.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n199\t                finding_id=str(request.get(\"finding_id\") or finding_id):\n212\t        raise GovernanceError(\n213\t            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n214\t            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n215\t        )\n216\t\n217\t    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n218\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n219\t        raise GovernanceError(\n220\t            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n221\t        )\n222\t    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n223\t    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n224\t    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n225\t    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n226\t    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n227\t    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n228\t    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n229\t    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n230\t    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n231\t    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n232\t    if not judge_id:\n233\t        raise GovernanceError(\n234\t            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n235\t            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n236\t        )\n237\t    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n238\t        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n239\t        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n240\t        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n241\t        raise GovernanceError(\n242\t            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n243\t        )\n244\t\n245\t    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n246\t    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n247\t    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n248\t    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n249\t\n250\t    note = (\n251\t        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n252\t        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n253\t        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n254\t        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n255\t    )\n256\t    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n257\t        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n258\t\n259\t    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n260\t    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n261\t\n262\t    row = record_operator_feedback(\n263\t        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n264\t        run_id=str(run_id),\n265\t        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n266\t        verdict=verdict,\n267\t        severity=severity,\n268\t        note=note,\n269\t        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n270\t        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n271\t        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n272\t        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n273\t The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n276\t        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n277\t        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n278\t        # predate the stamp.\n279\t        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),\n280\t        prompt_hash=verdict_block.get(\"prompt_hash\"),\n281\t        confidence=float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None,\n282\t        rationale=str(note),\n283\t        evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n284\t        # D1 — group identity comes from the MINT (judge_fanout's canonical\n285\t        # `judge:<tool>:<run>:<finding>`), never from the judge's own payload:\n286\t        # a judge echoing its request id as group id split one finding's two\n287\t        # verdicts across two groups-of-one, so the real disagreement on\n288\t        # anthropic.provider.ts:3 never met itself and never escalated.\n289\t        judgment_group_id=request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"judgment_group_id\"),\n290\t        # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — the fingerprint follows the SAME D1 doctrine as\n291\t        # the group id above: mint first, envelope only as fallback. Return whichever exists.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), dict):\n        return dict(details[\"verdict\"])\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"consensus\"), dict):\n        return dict(details[\"consensus\"])\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), str):\n        # Flat shape (ORPHAN-HIGH-629 sibling): the judge contract file says\n        # BOTH \"Return JSON with: verdict, judge_id, confidence...\" and\n        # \"details.verdict.rationale\". The first live judge run answered\n        # flat, was ACCEPTED by the envelope gate, and dropped HERE with\n        # \"expected verdict ..., got None\" — a verdict lost between two\n        # halves of one document. Honour both spellings; the envelope gate\n        # stays the only authority on validity.\n        return {\n            key: details.get(key)\n            for key in (\n                \"verdict\", \"tool_id\", \"run_id\", \"finding_id\", \"judge_id\",\n                \"model\", \"prompt_hash\", \"confidence\", \"rationale\",\n                \"evidence_refs\", \"judgment_group_id\", \"severity\",\n            )\n            if details.get(key) is not None\n        }\n    return {}\n\n\ndef validate_judge_response(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — the judge output contract, checkable BEFORE accept.\n\n    The second sealed night accepted 12 judge results whose envelopes never\n    carried a verdict the bridge could read (\"expected verdict ..., got\n    None\") — each one an accepted row that could never fold, burning bridge\n    retries toward permanent_fail. This is the SINGLE definition of what a\n    judge response must carry; ``record_judge_verdict_from_response`` raises\n    on exactly this list, and the executor's pre-submit gate releases the\n    claim on it instead of sealing an unfoldable result.\n\n    Returns [] for non-judge roles: the contract binds judges only.\n    \"\"\"\n    role = response.get(\"role\")\n    if not is_judge_role(role):\n        return []\n    errors: list[str] = []\n    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n    if not verdict_block:\n        return [\"judge_verdict:absent\"]\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's contract says \"return\n        # details.consensus, OR the uncertainty reason when the consensus\n        # gate cannot be met\", and the deterministic engine it mirrors\n        # emits exactly that non-verdict outcome. The live arbiter obeyed\n        # and this contract burned the claim as invalid:None — fourth\n        # mint-vs-law instance. Uncertainty is a VALID arbitration\n        # outcome, for the arbiter alone, with a reason from the closed\n        # vocabulary; binary judges stay bound to binary verdicts.\n        reason = verdict_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n        if (\n            role == \"consensus_arbitration\"\n            and verdict is None\n            and reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS\n        ):\n            pass\n        else:\n            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.verdict:invalid:{verdict!r}\")\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_tool_run_or_finding_id\")\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_judge_identity\")\n    elif str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:executor_shaped_judge_identity\")\n    return errors\n\n\ndef record_judge_verdict_from_response(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Translate an ACCEPTED judge response into an ai_judge feedback row.\n\n    Returns the persisted row, or None when the envelope role is not a\n    judge role (the bridge is a no-op for non-judge submissions).\n\n    Raises GovernanceError when the response is missing the data the\n    consensus engine requires (tool_id / run_id / finding_id come from\n    the request envelope's optional fields; verdict + severity from\n    the response.details).\n    \"\"\"\n    role = response.get(\"role\")\n    if not is_judge_role(role):\n        return None\n\n    # Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — one contract, two consumers: the executor's\n    # pre-submit gate checks exactly this list and releases instead of\n    # sealing an unfoldable accepted result; the bridge raises on it so a\n    # row that somehow slipped past still fails loudly, never half-folds.\n    contract_errors = validate_judge_response(request=request, response=response)\n    if contract_errors:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge contract violation: \" + \"; \".join(contract_errors)\n        )\n\n    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n\n    # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's legitimate non-verdict outcome\n    # folds through the SAME producer, ledger and idempotent escalation_id\n    # as the deterministic engine, so one sweep drains both lanes into one\n    # HUMAN_REQUIRED record. It is NOT a feedback verdict: an uncertain\n    # arbitration must never write an ai_judge row.\n    if role == \"consensus_arbitration\":\n        arb_block = _verdict_field(details)\n        arb_reason = arb_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n        if arb_block.get(\"verdict\") is None and arb_reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS:\n            from .feedback_store import record_consensus_uncertainty\n\n            row = record_consensus_uncertainty(\n                tool_id=str(request.get(\"tool_id\") or arb_block.get(\"tool_id\") or \"\"),\n                run_id=str(request.get(\"run_id\") or arb_block.get(\"run_id\") or \"\"),\n                finding_id=str(request.get(\"finding_id\") or arb_block.get(\"finding_id\") or \"\"),\n                group_id=str(request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or arb_block.get(\"group_id\") or \"\"),\n                reason=str(arb_reason),\n                cycle_id=request.get(\"cycle_id\"),\n                base_dir=base_dir,\n            )\n            return {\"kind\": \"consensus_uncertainty\", **row}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires tool_id, run_id, finding_id in request or response.details.verdict; \"\n            f\"got tool_id={tool_id!r}, run_id={run_id!r}, finding_id={finding_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge expected verdict in {FEEDBACK_VERDICTS}, got {verdict!r}\"\n        )\n    severity = _coerce_severity(verdict_block.get(\"severity\") or details.get(\"severity\"))\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — judge identity. `response[\"agent_id\"]` is the\n    # EXECUTOR's identity (`ci-executor:gha-<run>`), not the judge's: reading\n    # it collapsed every judge drained by one workflow run into a single\n    # voter (consensus structurally impossible) while judges drained by\n    # DIFFERENT runs counted as distinct voters with meaningless identity.\n    # The judge's real identity is the subagent the executor invoked\n    # (`details.agent_subagent_type`, force-stamped by ci_executor); the\n    # mock lane and legacy envelopes carry it as `verdict.judge_id`.\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"judge bridge requires details.agent_subagent_type \"\n            \"(or details.verdict.judge_id)\"\n        )\n    if str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        # Refuse loudly instead of silently repairing: an executor-shaped\n        # identity means the producer regressed, and a repaired row would\n        # hide that while still poisoning per-judge calibration.\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"judge bridge refuses executor-shaped judge identity: {judge_id!r}\"\n        )\n\n    # The unit-interval gate, not a bare isinstance: True is an int and\n    # float(\"nan\") is a float to isinstance, and both would ride a judge\n    # verdict into calibration as if they were probabilities.\n    confidence = confidence_in_unit_interval(verdict_block.get(\"confidence\"))\n\n    note = (\n        verdict_block.get(\"rationale\")\n        or verdict_block.get(\"note\")\n        or response.get(\"rationale\")\n        or \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n    )\n    if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n        note = \"envelope-routed judge submission\"\n\n    raw_evidence_refs = verdict_block.get(\"evidence_refs\") or response.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n    evidence_refs = [str(r) for r in raw_evidence_refs if isinstance(r, str) and r.strip()]\n\n    row = record_operator_feedback(\n        tool_id=str(tool_id),\n        run_id=str(run_id),\n        finding_id=str(finding_id),\n        verdict=verdict,\n        severity=severity,\n        note=note,\n        source_type=\"ai_judge\",\n        judge_id=str(judge_id),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — mint-first doctrine, same as judgment_group_id\n        # and finding_fingerprint above: the model the DISPATCHER resolved\n        # (`details.agent_dispatch_model`, force-stamped by ci_executor from\n        # the runtime profile) outranks the judge's own verdict.model\n        # self-report. The anchor distinct-model count must not trust a\n        # string the judged agent wrote about itself; the self-report\n        # survives only as the fallback for mock/legacy envelopes that\n        # predate the stamp.\n        model=details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\") or verdict_block.get(\"model\"),\n        prompt_hash=verdict_block.get(\"prompt_hash\"),\n        confidence=float(confidence) if confidence is not None else None,\n        rationale=str(note),\n        evidence_refs=evidence_refs,\n        # D1 — group identity comes from the MINT (judge_fanout's canonical\n        # `judge:<tool>:<run>:<finding>`), never from the judge's own payload:\n        # a judge echoing its request id as group id split one finding's two\n        # verdicts across two groups-of-one, so the real disagreement on\n        # anthropic.provider.ts:3 never met itself and never escalated.\n        judgment_group_id=request.get(\"judgment_group_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"judgment_group_id\"),\n        # ORPHAN-HIGH-765 — the fingerprint follows the SAME D1 doctrine as\n        # the group id above: mint first, envelope only as fallback. On live\n        # state 7 of 8 ai_judge rows carried an empty fingerprint because\n        # the mint never carried it and no prompt asks the judge to echo it,\n        # so every consensus row settled from those rows inherited the empty\n        # key — and promote_consensus_findings silently skipped it forever.\n        finding_fingerprint=request.get(\"finding_fingerprint\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_fingerprint\"),\n        base_dir=base_dir,\n    )\n    return row","numLines":240,"startLine":60,"totalLines":388}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JSDBXPrHJwzFLuBjMASRzd","type":"tool_result","content":"2520\t        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n2521\t        \"request_id\": request_id,\n2522\t        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n2523\t        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n2524\t        \"role\": role,\n2525\t        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n2526\t    }\n2527\t\n2528\t    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n2529\t    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n2530\t        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n2531\t    else:\n2532\t        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n2533\t        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). The agent\n2534\t        # being invoked and replying is transport evidence, not a\n2535\t        # satisfaction judgment; the verdict belongs to an independent\n2536\t        # judge reading this matrix, never to the executor that carried\n2537\t        # the reply.\n2538\t        synthesized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n2539\t        excerpt = (agent_text or \"<agent produced no text>\").strip()\n2540\t        excerpt_short = excerpt[:240] + (\"...\" if len(excerpt) > 240 else \"\")\n2541\t        if must_satisfy:\n2542\t            for item in must_satisfy:\n2543\t                if not isinstance(item, dict):\n2544\t                    continue\n2545\t                cid = item.get(\"id\")\n2546\t                if not cid:\n2547\t                    continue\n2548\t                synthesized.append({\n2549\t                    \"id\": cid,\n2550\t                    \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n2551\t                    \"evidence_refs\": [],\n2552\t                    \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n2553\t                })\n2554\t        if not synthesized:\n2555\t            # Last-resort single-row matrix; agent_text is the only\n2556\t            # truthful evidence. Without this, the kernel rejects with\n2557\t            # evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty.\n2558\t            synthesized.append({\n2559\t                \"id\": f\"agent-text-{request_id[-8:]}\",\n2560\t                \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n2561\t                \"evidence_refs\": [],\n2562\t                \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n2563\t            })\n2564\t        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = synthesized\n2565\t\n2566\t    # Carry through any agent-supplied evidence_refs / details / notes.\n2567\t    for passthrough in (\"evidence_refs\", \"details\", \"notes\", \"plan_content\"):\n2568\t        if passthrough in extracted and extracted[passthrough] is not None:\n2569\t            envelope[passthrough] = extracted[passthrough]\n2570\t    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n2571\t        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n2572\t\n2573\t    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n2574\t    # forensic context post-submission.\n2575\t    details = envelope.get(\"details\")\n2576\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n2577\t        details = {}\n2578\t    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — FORCE-set, not setdefault: this field is now the\n2579\t    # judge's calibration identity (judgment_bridge reads it as judge_id),\n2580\t    # and a setdefault would let the agent's own payload spoof another\n2581\t    # judge's identity into the per-judge precision ledger.\n2582\t    details[\"agent_subagent_type\"] = subagent_type\n2583\t    # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — the dispatch-resolved model, same doctrine as\n2584\t    # agent_subagent_type above: FORCE-set from the runtime profile the\n2585\t    # executor resolved at invocation time. The judge's own\n2586\t    # verdict.model self-report remains in the payload as data, but the\n2587\t    # kernel's anchor distinct-model count must not trust a string the\n2588\t    # judged agent wrote about itself — a misreported model silently\n2589\t    # satisfies or violates ANCHOR_MIN_DISTINCT_MODELS, which is exactly\n2590\t    # the guarantee that field exists to provide.\n2591\t    if dispatch_model:\n2592\t        details[\"agent_dispatch_model\"] = dispatch_model\n2593\t    details.setdefault(\"agent_text\", _safe_agent_text_excerpt(agent_text))\n2594\t    usage = extract_usage(parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout))\n2595\t    if usage is not None:\n2596\t        details.setdefault(\"claude_cli_usage\", usage)\n2597\t    envelope[\"details\"] = details\n2598\t\n2599\t    return envelope\n2600\t\n2601\t\n2602\tdef _rejected_result_recorded(submit_stdout: str) -> bool:\n2603\t    \"\"\"True when the submit step's answer is a kernel-recorded REJECTED result row.\n2604\t\n2605\t    `aria_kernel agent submit-result` prints its result document on stdout and\n2606\t    exits 1 for anything but `accepted`. When that document carries a result\n2607\t    row with status `rejected`, the kernel has already appended the row: the\n2608\t    claim is terminal (`_assert_lifecycle_mutation_allowed`) and the request\n2609\t    derives REJECTED (`derive_request_state`, results dominate). Anything else"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"311d41d8-3460-48c5-bd56-d1e1205b9591","uuid":"7563e1a1-c1b7-4888-999a-05cb1978b1f6","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:47:14.244Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-9d053dfeea6c/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n        \"request_id\": request_id,\n        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n        \"role\": role,\n        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n    }\n\n    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n    else:\n        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). The agent\n        # being invoked and replying is transport evidence, not a\n        # satisfaction judgment; the verdict belongs to an independent\n        # judge reading this matrix, never to the executor that carried\n        # the reply.\n        synthesized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        excerpt = (agent_text or \"<agent produced no text>\").strip()\n        excerpt_short = excerpt[:240] + (\"...\" if len(excerpt) > 240 else \"\")\n        if must_satisfy:\n            for item in must_satisfy:\n                if not isinstance(item, dict):\n                    continue\n                cid = item.get(\"id\")\n                if not cid:\n                    continue\n                synthesized.append({\n                    \"id\": cid,\n                    \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n                    \"evidence_refs\": [],\n                    \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n                })\n        if not synthesized:\n            # Last-resort single-row matrix; agent_text is the only\n            # truthful evidence. Without this, the kernel rejects with\n            # evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty.\n            synthesized.append({\n                \"id\": f\"agent-text-{request_id[-8:]}\",\n                \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n                \"evidence_refs\": [],\n                \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n            })\n        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = synthesized\n\n    # Carry through any agent-supplied evidence_refs / details / notes.\n    for passthrough in (\"evidence_refs\", \"details\", \"notes\", \"plan_content\"):\n        if passthrough in extracted and extracted[passthrough] is not None:\n            envelope[passthrough] = extracted[passthrough]\n    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n\n    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n    # forensic context post-submission.\n    details = envelope.get(\"details\")\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — FORCE-set, not setdefault: this field is now the\n    # judge's calibration identity (judgment_bridge reads it as judge_id),\n    # and a setdefault would let the agent's own payload spoof another\n    # judge's identity into the per-judge precision ledger.\n    details[\"agent_subagent_type\"] = subagent_type\n    # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — the dispatch-resolved model, same doctrine as\n    # agent_subagent_type above: FORCE-set from the runtime profile the\n    # executor resolved at invocation time. The judge's own\n    # verdict.model self-report remains in the payload as data, but the\n    # kernel's anchor distinct-model count must not trust a string the\n    # judged agent wrote about itself — a misreported model silently\n    # satisfies or violates ANCHOR_MIN_DISTINCT_MODELS, which is exactly\n    # the guarantee that field exists to provide.\n    if dispatch_model:\n        details[\"agent_dispatch_model\"] = dispatch_model\n    details.setdefault(\"agent_text\", _safe_agent_text_excerpt(agent_text))\n    usage = extract_usage(parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout))\n    if usage is not None:\n        details.setdefault(\"claude_cli_usage\", usage)\n    envelope[\"details\"] = details\n\n    return envelope\n\n\ndef _rejected_result_recorded(submit_stdout: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"True when the submit step's answer is a kernel-recorded REJECTED result row.\n\n    `aria_kernel agent submit-result` prints its result document on stdout and\n    exits 1 for anything but `accepted`. When that document carries a result\n    row with status `rejected`, the kernel has already appended the row: the\n    claim is terminal (`_assert_lifecycle_mutation_allowed`) and the request\n    derives REJECTED (`derive_request_state`, results dominate). ts-node\nimport { basename, dirname, join, relative, resolve } from 'node:path';\n\nimport ts from 'typescript';\n\nimport {\n  collectFiles,\n  filterFilesBySnapshot,\n  isArchivedWorkspacePath,\n  normalizeWorkspacePath,\n  readWorkspaceFile,\n  requireScanRoots,\n  resolveInsideWorkspace as resolveAdapterPath,\n  workspacePathExists,\n} from './adapter-fs';\n\ntype FindingRule =\n  | 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test'\n  | 'migration_without_test'\n  | 'security_source_without_security_test';\n\ninterface AdapterInput {\n  readonly roots?: readonly string[];\n  readonly allowlist?: readonly string[];\n  readonly includeWriteBoundaryFindings?: boolean;\n  readonly repo_snapshot?: { readonly allowed_paths?: readonly string[]; readonly snapshot_hash?: string; readonly repo_state_id?: string };\n}\n\ninterface EvidenceRef {\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterObservation {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly type: string;\n  readonly path?: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n  readonly name?: string;\n  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterFinding {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly rule: FindingRule;\n  readonly severity: 'medium' | 'high';\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n  readonly message: string;\n  readonly evidence: readonly EvidenceRef[];\n  readonly confidence?: number;\n  readonly actionability?: 'actionable' | 'review_required';\n  readonly review_reason?: string;\n  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface MemoryCandidate {\n  readonly belief_id: string;\n  readonly claim: string;\n  readonly confidence: number;\n  readonly evidence_refs: readonly string[];\n  readonly source_tool_id: string;\n}\n\ninterface AriaOutput AdapterObservation[];\n  readonly findings: AdapterFinding[];\n  readonly readPaths: string[];\n}\n\ninterface PathAlias {\n  readonly prefix: string;\n  readonly suffix: string;\n  readonly targets: readonly string[];\n}\n\n\nexport function analyzeTestGaps(input: AdapterInput, workspaceRoot = process.cwd()): AriaOutput {\n  const roots = requireScanRoots('test-gap-adapter', input.roots);\n  const allowlist = new Set((input.allowlist ?? []).map(normalizePath));\n  const files = roots\n    .map((root) => resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, root))\n    .filter((root) => workspacePathExists(root))\n    .flatMap((root) => collectSourceAndTestFiles(root));\n  const snapshotFiles = filterFilesBySnapshot(files, workspaceRoot, input);\n  const units = snapshotFiles.map((file) => readFileUnit(file, workspaceRoot));\n  const tests = units.filter((unit) => unit.isTest);\n  const sources = units.filter((unit) => !unit.isTest);\n  const sourcePaths = new Set(sources.map((source) => source.relativePath));\n  const pathAliases = readPathAliases(workspaceRoot);\n  const result: AnalysisResult = {\n    observations: [],\n    findings: [],\n    readPaths: units.map((unit) => unit.relativePath),\n  };\n\n  for (const test of tests) {\n    result.observations.push({\n      id: `test-gap-test-file:${test.relativePath}`,\n      type: 'test_gap_test_file',\n      path: test.relativePath,\n      line: 1,\n      name: basename(test.relativePath),\n      details: {\n        imports: importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).sort(),\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  let highRiskWithoutTest = 0;\n  let directMatchCount = 0;\n  let weakMatchCount = 0;\n  for (const source of sources) {\n    const risk = classifySourceRisk(source);\n    if (!risk.highRisk && !risk.securitySensitive && !risk.migrationHazard) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    const matchedTests = matchingTests(source, tests, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n    const weakMatchedTests = weakSymbolTests(source, tests).filter(\n      (test) => !matchedTests.some((matched) => matched.relativePath === test.relativePath),\n    );\n    directMatchCount += matchedTests.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;\n    weakMatchCount += weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;\n    const allowlisted = allowlist.has(source.relativePath);\n    result.observations.push({\n      id: `test-gap-source-file:${source.relativePath}`,\n      type: 'test_gap_source_file',\n      path: source.relativePath,\n      line: 1,\n      name: basename(source.relativePath),\n      details: {\n        riskClass: risk.riskClass,\n        highRisk: risk.highRisk,\n        securitySensitive: risk.securitySensitive,\n        migrationHazard: risk.migrationHazard,\n        writeBoundary: risk.writeBoundary,\n        matchedTests: matchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort(),\n        weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort(),\n        allowlisted,\n      },\n    });\n    if (allowlisted || matchedTests.length > 0) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (risk.migrationHazard) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `migration-without-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'migration_without_test',\n        severity: 'high',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'Hazardous migration has no adjacent or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.55 : 0.88,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (risk.securitySensitive) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `security-source-without-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'security_source_without_security_test',\n        severity: 'high',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'Security-sensitive source file has no adjacent or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.55 : 0.86,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (input.includeWriteBoundaryFindings === true && risk.highRisk && risk.writeBoundary) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `high-risk-source-without-adjacent-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test',\n        severity: 'medium',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'High-risk source file has no adjacent, sibling __tests__, or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.5 : 0.75,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  result.observations.push({\n    id: 'test-gap-coverage-summary',\n    type: 'test_gap_coverage_summary',\n    details: {\n      sourceFiles: sources.length,\n      testFiles: tests.length,\n      directMatchCount,\n      weakMatchCount,\n      highRiskWithoutTest,\n      unmatchedHighRiskCount: highRiskWithoutTest,\n      scanMode: 'import_graph_v1',\n    },\n  });\n\n  result.observations.sort(compareById);\n  result.findings.sort(compareById);\n  result.readPaths.sort();\n  const evidenceSources = Array.from(\n    new Set(result.findings.flatMap((finding) => finding.evidence.map((evidence) => evidence.path))),\n  ).sort();\n\n  return {\n    observations: result.observations,\n    findings: result.findings,\n    read_paths: Array.from(new Set(result.readPaths)).sort(),\n    evidence_sources: evidenceSources,\n    belief_candidates: [\n      {\n        belief_id: 'test-gap:high-risk-source-coverage-surface',\n        claim: 'high-risk backend, web security, and migration surfaces can be mapped to adjacent or importing test coverage signals',\n        confidence: 0.75,\n        evidence_refs: ['apps/**/*.ts', 'libs/**/*.ts', 'platform/libs/**/*.ts', 'web/**/*.tsx'],\n        source_tool_id: 'test-gap-adapter',\n      },\n    ],\n    cost_units: Array.from(new Set(result.readPaths)).length,\n    metadata: {\n      adapter: 'test-gap-adapter',\n      roots: roots.map(String).sort(),\n      files_scanned: snapshotFiles.length,\n      findings_count: result.findings.length,\n      allowlist_count: allowlist.size,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction classifySourceRisk(unit: FileUnit): SourceRisk {\n  const path = unit.relativePath;\n  const text = unit.text;\n  const riskClass = riskClassForPath(path);\n  const highRisk = /(\\.controller|\\.resolver|\\.handler|\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\.middleware|\\.interceptor)\\.ts$/.test(path);\n  const securitySensitive = /dangerouslySetInnerHTML|DOMPurify|sanitizeHtml|@Public\\b|AuthGuard|TenantGuard|PermissionGuard/i.test(text)\n    || /(\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\/auth\\/|\\/security\\/|\\/permission)/i.test(path);\n  const writeBoundary = /@(Post|Put|Patch|Delete|Mutation)\\b|\\b(create|update|delete|remove|archive|approve|reject|reset|invite)\\w*\\s*\\(/i.test(text);\n  const migrationHazard = /\\/migrations\\/.*\\.ts$/.test(path)\n    && /\\b(DROP|DELETE|TRUNCATE|ALTER\\s+TYPE|ENABLE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY|FORCE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY)\\b/i.test(text);\n  return { riskClass, highRisk, securitySensitive, migrationHazard, writeBoundary };\n}\n\nfunction riskClassForPath(path: string): string {\n  if (path.includes('/migrations/')) {\n    return 'migration';\n  }\n  // match[1] is narrowed explicitly so the file stays clean under\n  // --noUncheckedIndexedAccess (the capture group is structurally guaranteed,\n  // but the index signature cannot prove it to the compiler).\n  const match = path.match(/\\.([a-z-]+)\\.(ts|tsx)$/);\n  if (match?.[1] !== undefined) {\n    return match[1];\n  }\n  if (path.endsWith('.tsx')) {\n    return 'ui_component';\n  }\n  return 'source';\n}\n\nfunction matchingTests(\n  source: FileUnit,\n  tests: readonly FileUnit[],\n  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n): readonly FileUnit[] {\n  const sourceBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(source.relativePath);\n  const sourceDir = dirname(source.relativePath);\n  return tests.filter((test) => {\n    const testBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(test.relativePath);\n    if (testBase === sourceBase) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (dirname(test.relativePath) === sourceDir && testBase.startsWith(sourceBase)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (dirname(test.relativePath).endsWith(`${sourceDir}/__tests__`) && testBase.includes(sourceBase)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    return importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).some((specifier) => {\n      const resolved = resolveImportSpecifier(test.relativePath, specifier, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n      return resolved === source.relativePath;\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction weakSymbolTests(source: FileUnit, tests: readonly FileUnit[]): readonly FileUnit[] {\n  const symbolNames = exportedSymbols(source.sourceFile);\n  if (symbolNames.length === 0) {\n    return [];\n  }\n  return tests.filter((test) => symbolNames.some((symbolName) => symbolName.length > 2 && test.text.includes(symbolName)));\n}\n\nfunction basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(path: string): string {\n  return basename(path)\n    .replace(/\\.(spec|test)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n    .replace(/\\.(controller|resolver|handler|guard|strategy|middleware|interceptor|service|entity|module|component)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n    .replace(/\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '');\n}\n\nfunction importedSpecifiers(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n  const imports: string[] = [];\n  for (const statement of sourceFile.statements) {\n    if (ts.isImportDeclaration(statement) && ts.isStringLiteral(statement.moduleSpecifier)) {\n      imports.push(statement.moduleSpecifier.text);\n    }\n  }\n  return imports;\n}\n\nfunction resolveImportSpecifier(\n  importingPath: string,\n  specifier: string,\n  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n): string | undefined {\n  const candidates: string[] = [];\n  if (specifier.startsWith('.')) {\n    candidates.push(normalizePath(join(dirname(importingPath), specifier)));\n  } else {\n    for (const alias of pathAliases) {\n      if (!specifier.startsWith(alias.prefix) || !specifier.endsWith(alias.suffix)) {\n        continue;\n      }\n      const middle = specifier.slice(alias.prefix.length, specifier.length - alias.suffix.length);\n      for (const target of alias.targets) {\n        candidates.push(target.replace('*', middle));\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  for (const candidate of candidates.flatMap(candidatePathVariants)) {\n    if (sourcePaths.has(candidate)) {\n      return candidate;\n    }\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nfunction candidatePathVariants(candidate: string): string[] {\n  const normalized = normalizePath(candidate).replace(/^\\.\\//, '');\n  return [\n    normalized,\n    `${normalized}.ts`,\n    `${normalized}.tsx`,\n    `${normalized}/index.ts`,\n    `${normalized}/index.tsx`,\n  ];\n}\n\nfunction readPathAliases(workspaceRoot: string): readonly PathAlias[] {\n  const path = resolve(workspaceRoot, 'tsconfig.base.json');\n  if (!workspacePathExists(path)) {\n    return [];\n  }\n  try {\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(readWorkspaceFile(path)) as {\n      compilerOptions?: { paths?: Record<string, readonly string[]> };\n    };\n    return Object.entries(parsed.compilerOptions?.paths ?? {}).map(([key, targets]) => {\n      const starIndex = key.indexOf('*');\n      return {\n        prefix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(0, starIndex) : key,\n        suffix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(starIndex + 1) : '',\n        targets: targets.map((target) => normalizePath(target)),\n      };\n    });\n  } catch {\n    return [];\n  }\n}\n\nfunction exportedSymbols(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n  const symbols = new Set<string>();\n  visit(sourceFile, (node) => {\n    if ((ts.isClassDeclaration(node) || ts.isFunctionDeclaration(node) || ts.isInterfaceDeclaration(node) || ts.isTypeAliasDeclaration(node)) && node.name) {\n      const modifiers = ts.canHaveModifiers(node) ? ts.getModifiers(node) ?? [] : [];\n      if (modifiers.some((modifier) => modifier.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ExportKeyword)) {\n        symbols.add(node.name.text);\n      }\n    }\n  });\n  return Array.from(symbols).sort();\n}\n\nfunction readFileUnit(path: string, workspaceRoot: string): FileUnit {\n  const text = readWorkspaceFile(path);\n  return {\n    path,\n    relativePath: normalizePath(relative(workspaceRoot, path)),\n    text,\n    sourceFile: ts.createSourceFile(path, text, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true),\n    isTest: isTestFile(path),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction collectSourceAndTestFiles(root: string): readonly string[] {\n  return collectFiles(root, {\n    extensions: ['.ts', '.tsx'],\n    // E13 FP class (2): archived corpus (e.g. migrations/.archive/<timestamp>/)\n    // is dead code — it can neither need test coverage nor PROVIDE it (an\n    // archived spec must not satisfy a live source), so it is excluded from\n    // the scan entirely rather than special-cased at finding time.\n    includeFile: (name, path) => !name.endsWith('.d.ts') && !isArchivedWorkspacePath(path),\n  });\n}\n\nfunction isTestFile(path: string): boolean {\n  const normalized = normalizePath(path);\n  return /(__tests__|\\.spec\\.|\\.test\\.)/.test(normalized);\n}\n\nfunction resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot: string, requestedPath: string): string {\n  return resolveAdapterPath(workspaceRoot, requestedPath);\n}\n\nfunction visit(node: ts.Node, visitor: (node: ts.Node) => void): void {\n  visitor(node);\n  ts.forEachChild(node, (child) => visit(child, visitor));\n}\n\nfunction compareById(a: { readonly id: string }, b: { readonly id: string }): number {\n  return a.id.localeCompare(b.id);\n}\n\nfunction normalizePath(path: string): string {\n  return normalizeWorkspacePath(path);\n}\n\nfunction readStdin(): Promise<string> {\n  return new Promise((resolvePromise, reject) => {\n    let input = '';\n    process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');\n    // setEncoding('utf8') makes every chunk a string at runtime, but the\n    // stream's declared chunk type stays `string | Buffer` — concatenating the\n    // union is what the type checker rejects. Narrow at the boundary rather\n    // than widening `input` (kernel-dead-wire-adapter is the converged shape).\n    process.stdin.on('data', (chunk: string | Buffer) => {\n      input += typeof chunk === 'string' ? chunk : chunk.toString('utf8');\n    });\n    process.stdin.on('end', () => resolvePromise(input));\n    process.stdin.on('error', reject);\n  });\n}\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  const rawInput = await readStdin();\n  const input = rawInput.trim().length > 0 ? (JSON.parse(rawInput) as AdapterInput) : {};\n  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(analyzeTestGaps(input))}\\n`);\n}\n\nif (require.main === module) {\n  main().catch((error: unknown) => {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    process.stderr.write(`${message}\\n`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n}\n","numLines":494,"startLine":1,"totalLines":494}}}

Fenced ```json``` blocks containing a JSON object.\n2459\t      2. Last balanced top-level {...} block in the text.\n2460\t\n2461\t    Returns the parsed dict or None when no JSON is recoverable.\n2462\t    \"\"\"\n2463\t    # Pass 1: fenced JSON blocks — prefer the LAST one (agents typically\n2464\t    # narrate first then close with the envelope).\n2465\t    matches = _FENCED_JSON_RE.findall(text)\n2466\t    for body in reversed(matches):\n2467\t        try:\n2468\t            data = json.loads(body)\n2469\t        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n2470\t            continue\n2471\t        if isinstance(data, dict):\n2472\t            return data\n2473\t    # Pass 2: balanced-brace scan from end-of-text for the last {...} block.\n2474\t    depth = 0\n2475\t    end_idx = -1\n2476\t    for i in range(len(text) - 1, -1, -1):\n2477\t        ch = text[i]\n2478\t        if ch == \"}\":\n2479\t            if depth == 0:\n2480\t                end_idx = i\n2481\t            depth += 1\n2482\t        elif ch == \"{\":\n2483\t            depth -= 1\n2484\t            if depth == 0 and end_idx > 0:\n2485\t                candidate = text[i : end_idx + 1]\n2486\t                try:\n2487\t                    data = json.loads(candidate)\n2488\t                except json.JSONDecodeError:\n2489\t                    end_idx = -1\n2490\t                    continue\n2491\t                if isinstance(data, dict):\n2492\t                    return data\n2493\t    return None\n2494\t\n2495\t\n2496\tdef _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n2497\t    *,\n2498\t    raw_stdout: str,\n2499\t    request_id: str,\n2500\t    claim_id: str,\n2501\t    agent_id: str,\n2502\t    role: str,\n2503\t    subagent_type: str,\n2504\t    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n2505\t    dispatch_model: str | None = None,"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"311d41d8-3460-48c5-bd56-d1e1205b9591","uuid":"7c706148-45e5-4fb4-9e6b-f5ed1ee6be2e","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:48:56.118Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-9d053dfeea6c/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"        return\n\n\n# Regex tuned for ```json ... ``` fenced blocks anywhere in the agent\n# text. Re-used by _extract_envelope_json to find the envelope payload.\n_FENCED_JSON_RE = re.compile(r\"```(?:json)?\\s*(\\{.*?\\})\\s*```\", re.DOTALL)\n\n\ndef _extract_envelope_json(text: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Find the agent's embedded envelope JSON in a natural-language reply.\n\n    Scan order (first match wins):\n      1. Fenced ```json``` blocks containing a JSON object.\n      2. Last balanced top-level {...} block in the text.\n\n    Returns the parsed dict or None when no JSON is recoverable.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pass 1: fenced JSON blocks — prefer the LAST one (agents typically\n    # narrate first then close with the envelope).\n    matches = _FENCED_JSON_RE.findall(text)\n    for body in reversed(matches):\n        try:\n            data = json.loads(body)\n        except json.JSONDecodeError:\n            continue\n        if isinstance(data, dict):\n            return data\n    # Pass 2: balanced-brace scan from end-of-text for the last {...} block.\n    depth = 0\n    end_idx = -1\n    for i in range(len(text) - 1, -1, -1):\n        ch = text[i]\n        if ch == \"}\":\n            if depth == 0:\n                end_idx = i\n            depth += 1\n        elif ch == \"{\":\n            depth -= 1\n            if depth == 0 and end_idx > 0:\n                candidate = text[i : end_idx + 1]\n                try:\n                    data = json.loads(candidate)\n                except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                    end_idx = -1\n                    continue\n                if isinstance(data, dict):\n                    return data\n    return None\n\n\ndef _build_envelope_from_claude_output(\n    *,\n    raw_stdout: str,\n    request_id: str,\n    claim_id: str,\n    agent_id: str,\n    role: str,\n    subagent_type: str,\n    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    dispatch_model: str | None = None,","numLines":60,"startLine":2446,"totalLines":5265}}}
