apps/hr-service/src/finance/resolvers/hr-finance.resolver.ts, (a) it is security-sensitive and (b) no adjacent or importing test covers it. Both were checked against repository content at 1e6e99f7e, never against the adapter's output or prior ARIA reports. The excerpt hash was not recomputed; the file was re-read in full (307 lines) and lines 1-209 match the excerpt verbatim. (a) Security-sensitive, verified on substance: the resolver is the authorization boundary for salary and payroll data. Class-level @UseGuards(GqlAuthGuard) at line 73; tenant and user identity sourced from the JWT with UnauthorizedException on absence at lines 81-95; @UseGuards(RolesGuard)+@Roles on every field (first at 107-108); @RequireTenantPermission('hr_finance:view_salary') gating hrLabourCost at line 121; a resolver-local hasResourcePermission branch at line 148 that decides whether hrFinanceSummary discloses per-department salary; TENANT_ADMIN-only archive/restore/settings mutations at lines 271, 284, 297. The adapter's classifier fires on the AuthGuard token (test-gap-adapter.ts:271); the code justifies the label independently of the regex. (b) No test signal, verified by exhausting every branch of the adapter's matcher (test-gap-adapter.ts:296-328, semantics read from source): (1) no *.spec/*.test file with basename hr-finance exists anywhere in the repo (Glob **/hr-finance*.{spec,test}.{ts,tsx} returned nothing); (2) resolvers/ holds no spec; (3) there is no resolvers/__tests__/ directory, and apps/hr-service/src/finance/__tests__/ holds only labour-cost-calculator.spec.ts and merge-department-costs.spec.ts, which test pure calculators; (4) the only importer of hr-finance.resolver in the repo is hr-finance.module.ts:41, so no test imports it; (5) no test text contains the sole exported symbol HrFinanceResolver, so the weak-symbol escape hatch is empty too, matching the adapter's own 0.86/actionable grading. Two indirect signals were examined and do not cover this resolver's security behaviour: tests/invariants/hr-graphql-fe-be-parity.spec.ts:76 uses hrLabourCost only as an extractor canary (schema parity, no authorization assertion), and tests/invariants/tenant-permission-guard-adoption.spec.ts:58-82 asserts only that hr-service's app.module.ts registers TenantPermissionGuard, not that any field carries the decorator. Why it matters: the gating here is decorator metadata plus one inline permission branch, and nothing at build or test time asserts that hrLabourCost keeps @RequireTenantPermission, that archive/restore/settings stay TENANT_ADMIN-only, or that hrFinanceSummary withholds salary when the caller lacks hr_finance:view_salary. Deleting any of those lines compiles and passes the affected suite, so a MODULE_MANAGER without the salary capability would read payroll figures with a green CI. Downstream surface: the HR finance tab, whose operations file maps 1:1 to this resolver (web/modules/hr-module/src/graphql/finance.operations.ts:4). Recommended root-cause fix (Tier 3, make it detectable): add apps/hr-service/src/finance/resolvers/__tests__/hr-finance.resolver.spec.ts with mocked CommandBus/QueryBus that asserts the guard, role and tenant-permission metadata on each field via Reflect metadata and exercises the includeSalary=false path of hrFinanceSummary; that spec also satisfies the adapter's __tests__ sibling branch (test-gap-adapter.ts:312). Residual uncertainty, hence 0.92 rather than 1.0: the adapter's scan roots are not in the request, so its exact file set is inferred; the repo-wide search performed here is a superset of any root set, and it found no candidate the adapter could have matched.\",\n      \"evidence_refs\": [\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/finance/resolvers/hr-finance.resolver.ts:73\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/finance/resolvers/hr-finance.resolver.ts:81\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/finance/resolvers/hr-finance.resolver.ts:121\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/finance/resolvers/hr-finance.resolver.ts:148\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/finance/resolvers/hr-finance.resolver.ts:271\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/finance/resolvers/hr-finance.resolver.ts:284\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/finance/resolvers/hr-finance.resolver.ts:297\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/finance/hr-finance.module.ts:41\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/finance/__tests__/labour-cost-calculator.spec.ts\",\n        \"apps/hr-service/src/finance/__tests__/merge-department-costs.spec.ts\",\n        \"tests/invariants/hr-graphql-fe-be-parity.spec.ts:76\",\n        \"tests/invariants/tenant-permission-guard-adoption.spec.ts:58\",\n        \"tests/invariants/tenant-permission-guard-adoption.spec.ts:82\",\n        \"tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts:271\",\n        \"tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts:296\",\n        \"tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts:322\",\n        \"web/modules/hr-module/src/graphql/finance.operations.ts:4\"\n      ]\n    },\n    \"provenance\": {\n      \"snapshot_sha\": \"1e6e99f7e07f80b8e8288967160449e55f82b850\",\n      \"tool_id_derivation\": \"finding id prefix and rule name are minted only by tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts:190-191, whose self-declared tool id is test-gap-adapter (lines 252, 257); Why it matters: the gating here is decorator metadata plus one inline permission branch, and nothing at build or test time asserts that hrLabourCost keeps @RequireTenantPermission, that archive/restore/settings stay TENANT_ADMIN-only, or that hrFinanceSummary withholds salary when the caller lacks hr_finance:view_salary. Deleting any of those lines compiles and passes the affected suite, so a MODULE_MANAGER without the salary capability would read payroll figures with a green CI. Downstream surface: the HR finance tab, whose operations file maps 1:1 to this resolver (web/modules/hr-module/src/graphql/finance.operations.ts:4).
